### [37] [MT-WAM: Reorienting the One-Pass Predictive Representation Toward Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21474)
*Yiguang Yang,Jiankun Peng,Xiaoming Wang,Yiran Zhang,Zhibo Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MT-WAM，通过引入未来轨迹和视觉特征监督增强视频动作共训练，在无需生成未来视频的情况下显著提升机器人控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Fast-WAM方法虽通过视频-动作共训练提升控制能力，但其未来观测预测未明确优先处理控制所需的关键动态变化和视觉结构信息。

Method: 保留原有训练目标，增加对二维点轨迹和视觉特征的互补监督；构建轻量级双流分支（运动流提供动态条件，视觉流捕捉空间结构），使用结构化注意力掩码隔离双流交互；推理时仅使用单次重规划的视频和运动缓存，跳过未来视频生成。

Result: LIBERO基准成功率98.2%，LIBERO-Plus达73.7%（较Fast-WAM提升23.8个百分点）；RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand随机任务成功率从6.30%提升至19.40%；四项真实世界任务平均成功率从67.0%提升至77.8%。

Conclusion: MT-WAM通过精细化的未来状态监督机制，有效增强了视频骨干网络对动态和空间结构的表征能力，显著提升了机器人在复杂视觉条件下的动作生成性能与控制鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fast-WAM shows that video-action co-training improves control without generating future video at inference, making the representation from a single video diffusion Transformer forward central to action generation. However, future-observation prediction does not explicitly prioritize the future dynamics and visual structure needed for control. We present MT-WAM, which retains the original training objectives and adds complementary supervision for future two-dimensional point trajectories and visual features. A lightweight dual-stream branch copied from the video backbone's final blocks provides target-specific processing, while a structured attention mask prevents cross-stream attention. Motion-stream tokens supply additional dynamics conditions to the action expert. Future visual-feature prediction provides supervision in a feature space that captures object and spatial structure. This supervision trains the video backbone to provide more informative visual context for action generation under changing visual conditions, without adding visual-feature-stream tokens to action conditioning. At inference, MT-WAM uses video and motion caches computed once per replan and skips future-video prediction. Without additional embodied policy pretraining, MT-WAM achieves 98.2% success on LIBERO and 73.7% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding Fast-WAM by 23.8 percentage points on the latter. On RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand, Random success increases from 6.30% to 19.40%; across four real-world tasks, average success increases from 67.0% to 77.8%.

</details>


### [38] [OpenSAL360: Open-Source Crowdsourcing Platform for Omnidirectional Video Saliency Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21480)
*Alexey Bryncev,Andrey Moskalenko,Kira Shilovskaya,Ivan Kosmynin,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenSAL360平台，通过众包方式低成本高效采集360度视频显著性数据，发布包含500个视频、2000+标注者的大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决VR眼动追踪数据采集成本高、复杂且难以大规模扩展的问题，以支持视口自适应流媒体、压缩等沉浸式应用。

Method: 开发无需专用硬件的众包协议（仅需屏幕、鼠标和网络），对7个VR眼动数据集进行验证，并开展接口及预处理/后处理参数的消融实验。

Result: 验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性，构建了目前该领域最大的公开显著性数据集（500个全景视频，2000+标注者），并开源了OpenSAL360平台。

Conclusion: OpenSAL360为360度视频显著性预测提供了可扩展、低成本的解决方案，促进了该领域数据的积累与研究。

Abstract: Omnidirectional video saliency prediction plays an important role in many immersive multimedia applications, including viewport-adaptive streaming and compression, foveated rendering, mesh simplification, perceptual quality assessment. Yet progress in this area remains constrained by the cost and complexity of collecting eye-tracking data with VR headsets, which makes large-scale dataset creation difficult to extend. We present OpenSAL360, the first open-source platform for scalable, low-cost 360° video saliency collection. Unlike conventional VR-based protocols, it requires only a standard screen, mouse, and internet connection, enabling parallel saliency data collection from common crowdsourcing assessors without specialized hardware. We validate our collection protocol against seven well-established VR eye-tracking datasets and conduct ablation studies on key interface, pre-, and post-processing parameters. To demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed methodology, we collect and publicly release a saliency dataset covering 500 omnidirectional videos annotated by 2,000+ crowdsourcing assessors, making it, to the best of our knowledge, the largest dataset in this field. We make OpenSAL360 publicly available at https://github.com/msu-video-group/OpenSAL360.

</details>


### [39] [VoxelTTO: Voxel-Aligned Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Test-Time Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21498)
*Yibin Zhao,Yihan Pan,Yangwen Li,Jun Nan,Jianjun Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VoxelTTO，一种通过体素聚合和测试时优化（TTO）解决3D高斯泼溅中像素对齐缺陷及相机位姿误差问题的前馈重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS方法因像素-高斯对应关系导致过度重叠和伪影；预测相机位姿的不准确会导致新视角合成中的错位。

Method: 1. 将密集图像特征聚合为全局体素表示并解码高斯，打破像素-高斯对应；2. 引入测试时优化（TTO），利用位姿监督微调轻量级LoRA模块以适配已知相机参数；3. 使用随机实体体积渲染替代传统光栅化提升几何保真度。

Result: 在Replica、Tanks and Temples和DTU数据集上，相比 prior methods 显著提升了RGB-D新视角合成质量及相机位姿估计精度。

Conclusion: VoxelTTO通过解耦像素-高斯映射和优化策略，实现了从任意数量图像进行几何准确的3DGS场景重建。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods typically regress pixel-aligned Gaussian primitives, often causing excessive overlap and artifacts, while inaccuracies in predicted camera poses can lead to misalignment in novel-view synthesis (NVS). We present VoxelTTO, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing geometrically accurate 3DGS scenes from an arbitrary number of images and optional camera parameters. VoxelTTO aggregates dense image features into a global voxel representation and decodes Gaussians from voxel features, breaking the pixel-to-Gaussian correspondence. To exploit known camera parameters while keeping the pretrained visual foundation model (VFM) parameters frozen, we introduce test-time optimization (TTO) that adapts lightweight LoRA modules using pose supervision. We further replace vanilla 3DGS rasterization with stochastic solid volume rendering during training and inference, improving geometric fidelity. Training updates only the voxel-aligned Gaussian reconstruction modules, requiring 80 GPU hours. Experiments on Replica, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate improved RGB-D NVS and camera-pose estimation relative to prior methods.

</details>


### [40] [Adaptive World Memory 3D Foundation Model for Scalable 3D Mapping, Localization, and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21502)
*Tianchen Deng,Guole Shen,Yilin Shen,Wenhua Wu,Yilin Fang,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Shenghai Yuan,Wolfram Burgard,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种以记忆为中心的3D基础模型AWM-3DFM，通过自适应世界记忆机制解决现有模型在持久记忆、可扩展性和可渲染场景建模上的局限。该模型结合Transformer门控更新与时空调节，支持大规模地图构建、高精度定位、稠密重建及高斯渲染，并在多个基准测试中优于现有SLAM和重建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D基础模型虽然能从RGB图像实现泛化的几何推理，但在持久记忆能力、大规模扩展性以及可渲染的场景建模方面存在显著限制，难以满足机器人长期运行中对持续世界建模的需求。

Method: 核心是自适应世界记忆机制：1) 使用基于Transformer的门控更新控制递归记忆传播；2) 通过测试时的时空调节（Temporal-Spatial Regulation）管理长序列图像中的令牌级更新与遗忘，确保状态演化的一致性。为支持大规模映射，采用局部子图组织记忆，并集成渐进式映射跟踪、回环检测及基于SL(4)的全局优化以保持局部精度和全局一致性。最后通过高斯重建头将增强特征解码为可渲染原语。

Result: 在公共基准数据集和来自不同机器人平台的自收集数据集上进行了实验。结果表明，该方法在轨迹精度、重建完整性和渲染质量方面均优于现有的3D基础重建和SLAM基线方法。

Conclusion: 自适应记忆机制可作为持久性机器人世界建模的基础，统一了相机位姿估计、稠密点云重建和 photorealistic 渲染，证明了其在可扩展机器人感知中的有效性。代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models enable generalizable geometric reasoning from RGB images but remain limited in persistent memory, scalability, and renderable scene modeling. We present a memory-centric 3D foundation model for scalable robotic localization, reconstruction, and Gaussian rendering. Its core is an adaptive world memory mechanism that combines transformer-based gated updates with test-time temporal-spatial regulation. Learned gates control recurrent memory propagation, while temporal state evolution and spatial observation-state consistency regulate token-wise updates and forgetting over long image sequences. To support large-scale mapping, we organize memory into local submaps and integrate progressive mapping and tracking, loop closure, and SL(4)-based global refinement to maintain local accuracy and global consistency. A Gaussian reconstruction head decodes memory-enhanced features into renderable primitives, unifying camera pose estimation, dense point-cloud reconstruction, and photorealistic rendering within a single model. Experiments on public benchmarks and self-collected datasets from diverse robotic platforms demonstrate improved trajectory accuracy, reconstruction completeness, and rendering quality over existing 3D foundation reconstruction and SLAM baselines. These results support adaptive memory as a foundation for persistent robotic world modeling. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at \href{https://github.com/dtc111111/AWM-3DFM}{https://github.com/dtc111111/AWM-3DFM}.

</details>


### [41] [2D GauSS-MI: Efficient Active Scene Reconstruction with Balanced Visual and Geometric Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21516)
*Yuhan Xie,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于2D高斯溅射（2DGS）的主动重建框架，通过创新的2D GauSS-MI互信息度量平衡视觉与几何质量，在有限算力下实现高效实时重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有主动重建方法在平衡视觉/几何质量与实时计算效率之间的矛盾，特别是在机载计算资源受限场景下难以兼顾高质量重建与低延迟的问题。

Method: 1. 构建增量式RGB-D观测的高效在线2DGS映射流水线；
2. 开发概率可靠性模型以表征单个2D高斯溅射的视图依赖重建质量；
3. 提出2D GauSS-MI指标，利用2DGS显式表面方向评估候选视图的预期信息增益，指导主动视图选择。

Result: 在Replica数据集的8个场景中，相比三种最先进基线方法，本方法在保持具有竞争力的模型存储量的同时，显著降低了计算成本，并实现了视觉与几何重建质量的优越平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合概率可靠性模型与信息论度量，有效解决了主动重建中的效率与质量权衡问题，为资源受限环境下的实时高质量3D重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Active reconstruction requires efficient active view selection to achieve high-quality reconstruction within limited onboard computational resources. Existing methods face challenges in adequately balancing visual and geometric quality with the computational efficiency required for real-time operation. In this work, we present an active reconstruction framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS). We develop an efficient online 2DGS mapping pipeline for incremental RGB-D observations and introduce a probabilistic reliability model that characterizes the view-dependent reconstruction quality of individual 2D Gaussian splats. Building on this model, we formulate 2D Gaussian Splatting Shannon Mutual Information (2D GauSS-MI), a mutual-information-based metric that exploits the explicit surface orientation of 2DGS to evaluate the expected information gain of candidate views. The proposed metric enables active view selection to account for both visual and geometric reconstruction quality. We evaluate the proposed system against three state-of-the-art baselines on eight Replica scenes. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves a favorable balance between visual and geometric reconstruction quality with substantially lower computational cost and competitive model storage.

</details>


### [42] [VidOmni-Bench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Video Understanding via Spatio-Temporal Event Verification across Complexity and Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21521)
*Changbeen Kim,Junwon Chang,Kipyo Kim,Risa Shinoda,Kuniaki Saito,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VidOmni-Bench基准，用于评估Video-LLMs在密集视频字幕事件验证上的细粒度理解能力，揭示模型幻觉及验证弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖问答或字幕匹配，易受表面线索干扰且标注不全，难以可靠评估Video-LLMs的细粒度视频理解能力。

Method: 构建包含500个视频（涵盖5种复杂度、4秒至90分钟时长）的VidOmni-Bench；利用不同Video-LLMs生成密集字幕并获取人工验证的句子级标签，将含错误事件句子作为难负样本进行评估。

Result: (1) Video-LLMs在密集字幕生成中常出现幻觉描述；(2) 模型作为验证器时，难以可靠检测合理但错误的事件描述；(3) 模型弱点随视频复杂度和时长变化，呈现多样化的模型特定瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前Video-LLMs在细粒度事件理解和验证方面存在显著不足，其性能瓶颈与视频特性密切相关，需针对性改进。

Abstract: While Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong performance, reliably evaluating their fine-grained video understanding remains challenging. Existing benchmarks often rely on question answering or ground-truth caption matching, where models may succeed through superficial cues and incomplete annotations. To this end, we introduce VidOmni-Bench, a benchmark that requires models to verify whether each event in dense video captions is supported by the video. VidOmni-Bench consists of 500 videos spanning five complexity types and diverse durations from 4 seconds to 90 minutes. After collecting videos along these axes, we use diverse Video-LLMs to generate dense captions and obtain human-verified sentence-level labels, where sentences containing incorrect events serve as hard negatives for evaluation. Our experiments on VidOmni-Bench reveal three key findings: (i) Video-LLMs frequently generate hallucinated descriptions in dense video captioning; (ii) they also struggle as verifiers, failing to reliably detect plausible but incorrect event descriptions; and (iii) model weaknesses vary across video complexity and duration, revealing diverse, model-specific bottlenecks in current Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [Refine Then Fusion: Training-Free 3D Point Cloud Adaptation with Priority Refinement and Multi-Modal Knowledge Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21522)
*Hang Cheng,Yan Chen,Mingyu Fan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免训练的少样本3D识别框架RTF，通过联合建模类间相似性和类内稳定性来筛选判别性特征通道，并基于特征细化引起的分布偏移估计模态可靠性，实现自适应多模态融合及记忆缓存推理，在五个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练基础模型在少样本3D视觉任务中面临两大挑战：高维特征存在通道冗余和无关噪声，以及不同样本间模态可靠性差异大；直接聚合异构表示会忽视样本依赖的模态可靠性并掩盖判别线索。

Method: 提出Refine Then Fusion (RTF)框架：1) 通过联合建模类间相似度和类内稳定性识别判别性特征通道，解耦领域知识细化与预训练模型缓存表示；2) 引入可靠性感知融合机制，从特征细化诱导的分布偏移中估计样本级模态可靠性，进行自适应多模态聚合；3) 构建集成实例级支持特征与类级原型的记忆缓存以推断查询标签。

Result: 在五个基准测试上进行的大量实验表明，RTF一致优于单模态基线、部分融合变体及现有轻量级适配方法，在无梯度优化、无额外训练数据、无辅助训练或参数更新的情况下，实现了最先进的少样本3D识别性能。

Conclusion: RTF作为一种免训练框架，有效解决了少样本3D识别中的特征冗余和模态可靠性问题，显著提升了识别性能且无需额外的训练成本。

Abstract: Recent pre-trained foundation models provide rich multi-modal priors for downstream 3D vision tasks. However, the effectiveness of these representations in few-shot scenarios is limited by two fundamental challenges: High-dimensional features often contain substantial channel redundancy and task-irrelevant noise, while the reliability of different modalities varies across samples. Consequently, direct aggregation of heterogeneous representations overlooks sample-dependent modality reliability and may obscure the discriminative cues essential. To address these limitations, we propose Refine Then Fusion(RTF), a training-free framework for few-shot 3D recognition. RTF first identifies discriminative feature channels by jointly modeling inter-class similarity and intra-class stability, thereby decoupling domain-specific knowledge refinement from the cached representations of pre-trained models. It then introduces a reliability-aware fusion mechanism that estimates sample-wise modality reliability from the distribution shifts induced by feature refinement, enabling adaptive aggregation of multi-modal representations. Furthermore, RTF constructs a memory cache that integrates instance-level support features with class-level prototypes to infer query labels. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that RTF consistently outperforms single-modal baselines, partial-fusion variants, and existing lightweight adaptation methods, achieving state-of-the-art few-shot 3D recognition performance without gradient optimization, additional training data, auxiliary training, or parameter updates.

</details>


### [44] [Purification and Regulation: Comorbidity-Aware Multi-Label Few-Shot Learning for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21541)
*Ying-Chih Lin,Po-Chih Kuo,Yong-Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PPR框架，通过原型净化和调节解决医学图像多标签少样本学习中的原型污染及疾病相关性忽略问题，显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于度量的元学习方法在医学图像分析中存在两大局限：1. 传统原型生成混杂无关疾病信息导致原型污染；2. 忽视疾病间固有的内在相关性，仅关注类间可分性。

Method: 提出原型净化与调节（PPR）框架：1. 利用样本级共病评分进行原型净化，强调疾病特异性特征；2. 结合疾病级共病统计量自适应调节类间相似度，构建感知共病的嵌入空间。

Result: 在四个胸部X光基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，包括跨域评估，结果显示PPR一致优于最先进方法，显著提高了疾病检测能力，并展现出强大的泛化能力和临床适用性。

Conclusion: PPR通过捕捉纯净的疾病特征和类间关系，为医学图像分析中的可靠多标签少样本学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-label few-shot learning (MLFSL) remains a significant challenge in medical image analysis (MIA). Current metric-based meta-learning methods face two critical limitations in MIA. First, conventional prototype generation often entangles irrelevant disease information, leading to contaminated prototypes and degraded performance. Second, prior studies typically enforce inter-class separability in embedding space, largely neglecting the inherent correlations among diseases. To overcome these challenges, we propose Prototype Purification and Regulation (PPR), a novel MLFSL framework for MIA. PPR first performs prototype purification by leveraging sample-level comorbidity scores to emphasize disease-specific features, producing purified prototypes that better characterize each disease. Building upon these purified prototypes, PPR further addresses the underexplored problem of inter-class prototype distance in MIA by incorporating disease-level comorbidity statistics to adaptively regulate inter-class similarity, forming a comorbidity-aware embedding space. Overall, PPR sequentially enables the model to capture pure disease features and inter-class relationships for reliable MLFSL in MIA. Extensive experiments across four chest X-ray benchmark datasets, including cross-domain evaluation, show that PPR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, significantly improving disease detection while demonstrating robust generalization and clinical applicability.

</details>


### [45] [From Retrieval to Recognition:How Vision--Language Models Become OCR Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21543)
*Yuanxiang Huangfu,Hanmeng Zhong,Linqing Chen,Jeffrey Tiong Jee Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究揭示了通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的OCR能力并非通过开发全新的“阅读电路”，而是通过复用现有的文本检索/复制机制实现。全序列OCR本质上是一种密集的多模态“复制-粘贴”过程，且在从通用模型向OCR专家模型转变的过程中，核心神经元头部身份得以保留，仅功能权重发生重新分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用视觉-语言模型在获得专用OCR能力时，是构建了新的读取回路，还是复用了现有机制；重点考察全序列OCR场景下的机制起源。

Method: 采用基于证据的协议和留置因果干预方法，在GLM-OCR、MinerU2.5和PaddleOCR-VL-1.6中识别稀疏且稳定的OCR头部集合；通过与通用VLM中独立识别的文本检索/复制头部进行对比分析，以及基座模型与专业化模型的匹配比较，探究机制来源及演变。

Result: 1. 视觉OCR头部与通用VLM中的文本检索/复制头部存在强烈重叠（前20个头部交集达73.3%，Spearman相关系数0.677-0.886）。
2. 全序列OCR表现为密集的顺序多模态复制-粘贴操作，即反复检索视觉证据并路由至当前输出位置。
3. OCR专业化过程中，头部身份高度保留（每任务前20个头部中保留17-20个，秩相关系数0.874-0.942），但功能和因果强度发生了重新分布。

Conclusion: 通用VLM的OCR能力源于对现有文本处理机制的复用，而非全新电路的开发；模型向OCR专家演进时，主要通过调整已有神经元的功能强度和因果作用来实现专业化，而非改变其基本身份。

Abstract: Does a general vision--language model acquire specialized OCR ability by developing a new reading circuit or by reusing an existing mechanism? We address this question in the setting of full-sequence OCR, rather than local-answer retrieval. Using an evidence-grounded protocol with held-out causal interventions, we identify sparse and stable OCR-head sets in GLM-OCR, MinerU2.5, and PaddleOCR-VL-1.6. We then investigate the mechanistic origin of these OCR heads by comparing them with independently identified textual retrieval/copy heads in general VLMs. Across two general VLMs, visual OCR heads strongly overlap independently identified textual retrieval/copy heads, yielding untuned top-20 intersections of 73.3% and all-head Spearman correlations of 0.677-0.886. The overlap and causal interventions suggest that full-sequence OCR operates as dense sequential multimodal copy-and-paste, repeatedly retrieving visual evidence and routing it to the current output position. Finally, we examine how this shared circuit changes as a general VLM becomes an OCR specialist. Matched base-to-specialized comparisons show that OCR specialization largely preserves head identity, retaining 17-20 of the top 20 heads per task with all-head rank correlations of 0.874-0.942, while redistributing their functional and causal strengths.

</details>


### [46] [GestureFAR: Streaming Co-Speech Gesture Generation with Flow Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21576)
*Pinxin Liu,Haiyang Liu,Jiahao Luo,Junhua Huang,Chunhao Zou,Luchuan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GestureFAR，一种基于流自回归（flow-autoregressive）的框架，用于解决流式语音伴随手势生成中的实时性与真实感矛盾。通过因果连续运动潜变量建模和头部分流蒸馏技术，在保持因果性的同时显著提升生成质量与延迟的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式手势生成系统多采用自回归离散运动标记（tokens），这种将高维连续运动压缩至有限码本的设计限制了生成手势的真实感和多样性。需要在保持因果性（causality）的同时保留连续表达的丰富性，以满足具身对话代理对实时交互的需求。

Method: 1. **架构设计**：提出GestureFAR框架，使用Transformer建模流式音频-运动上下文，并采用因果连续运动潜变量（causal continuous motion latents）。每个token使用流匹配头（flow-matching head）从连续分布中采样下一个潜变量。
2. **优化策略**：引入“仅头部流蒸馏”（head-only flow distillation）策略，冻结因果主干网络，利用一致性和分布匹配目标将多步每token流头蒸馏为单次网络评估，从而消除实时交互的主要延迟瓶颈，同时保持Token因果性。

Result: 在BEAT2数据集上的实验表明，GestureFAR显著改善了具备流式能力方法中的质量-延迟权衡（quality-latency trade-off）。该方法在实现实时Token因果生成的同时，保持了高水平的手势生成质量。

Conclusion: GestureFAR通过结合流匹配与自回归机制，有效解决了流式手势生成中真实性、多样性与低延迟之间的平衡问题，为具身智能体的实时自然交互提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Generating natural co-speech gestures from streaming speech is essential for embodied conversational agents, where motion must be produced while a user is still speaking. Recent streaming gesture systems make online generation possible by autoregressing over discrete motion tokens, but this design compresses high-dimensional continuous motion into finite codebooks and can limit the realism and diversity of generated gestures. To preserve both causality and continuous expressiveness, we propose \textbf{GestureFAR}, a flow-autoregressive framework for streaming co-speech gesture generation. First, GestureFAR autoregresses over causal continuous motion latents, using a transformer to model streaming audio-motion context and a per-token flow-matching head to sample the next latent from a continuous distribution. Second, we introduce a head-only flow distillation strategy that freezes the causal backbone and distills the multi-step per-token flow head into a single network evaluation using consistency and distribution-matching objectives. This keeps the model token-causal while removing the main latency bottleneck for live interaction. Experiments on BEAT2 show that GestureFAR significantly improves the quality--latency trade-off among streaming-capable methods, preserving strong gesture quality while enabling real-time token-causal generation. Project Page: https://andypinxinliu.github.io/GestureFAR

</details>


### [47] [A benchmark dataset and baseline methods for four-dimensional STEM diffraction patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21593)
*Yuyan Guan,Haoran Zhang,Zian Mao,Antong Yang,Caifei Li,Jialong Wang,Chuying Ouyang,Hong Wang,Xiaoqin Zeng,Yujun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了4D-ImageNet，一个包含17.4万个衍射图案的大规模数据集（含实验与模拟数据），旨在解决4D-STEM数据量大且异构的问题，支持表征学习、斑点检测及跨域研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）虽然能提供空间分辨的倒易空间信息，但产生大量且异构的数据体积，缺乏标准化的大规模数据集用于算法开发和基准测试。

Method: 构建包含174,000个衍射图案的数据集：145,000个来自29次采集的实验数据（涵盖Ag, Au, CoO等样品，具有获取级标签和元数据），以及29,000个多切片模拟数据（涵盖13种晶体结构）。提供基于集合预测检测器的伪标签（直接束位置和布拉格盘中心），并设立分组混合域掩码重建基准以评估数据加载和评估的抗泄漏能力。

Result: 成功构建了涵盖多种材料、参数和模拟场景的大规模4D-STEM数据集，提供了高质量的标注信息和标准化的评估基准，实现了实验数据与模拟数据的混合域评估框架。

Conclusion: 4D-ImageNet为4D-STEM领域的表征学习、斑点检测、图案检索、取向分析及仿真到实验研究提供了重要的基础资源，促进了该领域数据驱动方法的发展。

Abstract: Four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) records a two-dimensional diffraction pattern at each electron-probe position, yielding spatially resolved reciprocal-space information but large, heterogeneous data volumes. Here we describe 4D-ImageNet, a collection of 174,000 diffraction patterns comprising 145,000 experimental patterns selected from 29 acquisitions and 29,000 multislice simulations. The experimental data cover acquisition-level labels for Ag, Au, mixed Au-Ag, CoO, Pd and ZnO specimens across multiple fields of view, scan dimensions, camera lengths and exposure times. Each acquisition contributes 5,000 quality-ranked patterns with source scan coordinates and acquisition metadata. A set-prediction detector provides model-derived pseudo-labels for the direct-beam position and Bragg-disk centres, with a confidence score for each disk. The simulation data cover 13 crystal structures and include Euler rotations, reciprocal-space sampling and approximate low-index beam directions. A grouped mixed-domain masked-reconstruction benchmark is provided to assess leakage-resistant loading and evaluation across experimental and simulated data. The dataset is intended for representation learning, disk detection, diffraction-pattern retrieval, orientation analysis and simulation-to-experiment studies.

</details>


### [48] [HAT: Hypothesis-Anchored Tracking for Video Monocular Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21597)
*André Lopo,Atabak Dehban,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HAT框架，利用帧间运动在CAD姿态假设中选择最优解，解决单目6-DoF位姿估计中的对称性混淆问题，实现无需目标特定训练的实时高精度跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非合作目标单目6-DoF位姿估计中，单一图像估算易混淆近对称航天器姿态，且错误跟踪可能持续累积误差的问题。

Method: 提出假设锚定跟踪（HAT）因果框架，保留竞争姿态历史，利用帧间运动在对齐和融合前选择锚定相对轨迹的姿态；结合稀疏锚点与位姿融合提供每帧估计，仅依赖校准RGB序列、度量CAD模型及目标区域，使用预训练网络无需微调。

Result: 在SPARK-2024、SwissCube、SHIRT等数据集评估：Mega-HAT相比独立MegaPose平均位姿误差降低9.4%，吞吐量提升3.76倍；Pico-HAT相比独立PicoPose误差降低23.9%，吞吐量提升2.42倍；YCB-Video验证了空间外领域的性能。

Conclusion: HAT框架通过引入时序因果选择和姿态锚定机制，显著提升了单目SLAM系统的位姿估计精度与计算效率，证明了其在无需重新训练情况下的泛化能力与实用性。

Abstract: Monocular 6-DoF pose estimation of non-cooperative targets is important for on-orbit servicing and debris removal. A single-image estimator can confuse near-symmetric spacecraft orientations, and tracking can preserve an incorrect pose. We present Hypothesis-Anchored Tracking (HAT), a causal framework that uses inter-frame motion to select among competing CAD-based pose hypotheses before alignment and fusion. Rather than independently choosing the highest-scoring hypothesis in each image, HAT retains competing orientation histories and selects a pose to anchor the relative trajectory estimated by monocular SLAM. Sparse anchors and pose fusion provide per-frame estimates after initialization without revising past outputs. The method requires only a calibrated RGB sequence, a metric CAD model, and target image regions, which can be supplied by detection or segmentation. The pretrained pose and SLAM networks require no target-specific training or fine-tuning. We evaluate two versions, Mega-HAT and Pico-HAT, using MegaPose and PicoPose, on SPARK-2024, SwissCube and SHIRT, with YCB-Video assessing performance outside the space domain. Using one temporal configuration per method, the arithmetic means of the four dataset-wise comparisons show 9.4% lower mean pose error and 3.76 times the sustained input FPS for Mega-HAT relative to independent MegaPose, and 23.9% lower mean pose error and 2.42 times the FPS for Pico-HAT relative to independent PicoPose. Mega-HAT ablations on SPARK and an offline reference examine component contributions and the effect of revising past estimates.

</details>


### [49] [Learned Parametric Emotion Editing: Real-Time Affective Filtering for On-Device Social Media Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21624)
*Musa Rochi,Marcel Schubert,Christoph Gebhardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于MobileNetV4和FiLM的情绪条件模型，通过单次前向传播（3.7毫秒）实时调整视觉内容的情感强度，替代耗时的迭代优化。该模型在用户研究中有效降低唤醒度且感知质量更高，并集成至Android应用实现Instagram视频60fps的实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对网络使用问题，现有干预措施（如时间限制、屏蔽）具有强制性且易被规避。旨在探索一种非强制性的替代方案，即通过调整视觉内容的情感强度来改善用户体验，解决现有图像情感优化方法因单图计算成本高（80秒）而无法实时部署的问题。

Method: 构建一个基于MobileNetV4骨干网络的模型，结合FiLM（Feature-wise Linear Modulation）进行情绪条件化，输出可微的全局变换参数。该方法通过单次前向传播预测情感转换，取代了先前每张图片需80秒的迭代优化过程。

Result: 1. 效率提升：将处理时间从每张图片80秒缩短至3.7毫秒的前向传播时间。2. 用户研究（N=54）：相比未编辑图像，模型显著降低了观看者的报告唤醒度，效果与灰度健康滤镜相当，但在感知质量评分上更高。3. 实际应用：成功集成到Android应用中，在Samsung Galaxy S23上实现Instagram视频的实时处理，帧率稳定在60 fps。

Conclusion: 通过学习而非优化的方法，实现了高效、低延迟的视觉内容情感强度自适应调整。该方法提供了一种比传统强制手段更柔和、更高质量的潜在干预途径，具备在实际移动设备上实时部署的可行性。

Abstract: Problematic internet use affects a growing share of the population, yet common interventions, e.g., time limits, blocking, forced breaks, are coercive and easily circumvented. We explore a less restrictive alternative: adapting the emotional intensity of visual content. Prior work has shown that optimization can steer an image's affective content, but its per-image optimization cost makes it impractical for real-time deployment. We instead learn a model that predicts this transformation in a single forward pass: a MobileNetV4 backbone with FiLM-based emotion conditioning outputs parameters for differentiable global transformations. This replaces prior iterative optimization (80 s per image) with a single 3.7 ms forward pass. In a user study (N = 54), the model reduced viewer-reported arousal relative to unedited images, comparably to the grayscale well-being filter, while being rated higher in perceived quality. We integrate the model into an Android app that adapts Instagram video in real time, sustaining 60 fps on a Samsung Galaxy S23.

</details>


### [50] [Detection is solved, delineation is not: what governs tooth segmentation on panoramic radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21628)
*Muhammad Rehan,Moaz Amjad,Syed Danial Ahmed,Mariam Adnan,Haider Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模数据集和严格控制变量的消融实验，发现自动牙分割与编号性能主要受输入分辨率主导，而非模型架构或解剖先验。高分辨率显著提升边界精度（mAP50-95），而不同架构间性能差异不显著。零样本迁移证实边界精度是核心瓶颈，建议未来工作应聚焦于提升分辨率和数据多样性，而非追求架构创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动牙齿分割和FDI编号对计算机辅助牙科诊断至关重要，但影响其性能的关键因素尚不明确。本研究旨在孤立并评估输入分辨率、网络架构和解剖先验等因素对性能的具体影响。

Method: 构建包含1,422张全景X光片和42,142个专家标注多边形的大型语料库，采用统一的评估协议进行对照实验。具体包括：1) 控制变量法测试640/1024/1280三种分辨率的影响；2) 比较基于查询的Transformer与单阶段检测器的性能及效率；3) 评估LoRA自适应编码器、可提示的基础分割器及全局最优解剖标签分配等干预措施的效果；4) 在独立多中心队列上进行零样本迁移验证。

Result: 1. 分辨率起决定性作用：随着分辨率从640提升至1280，mask mAP50-95显著上升（0.656至0.717），而mAP50保持稳定，表明分辨率主要提升边界精度而非检测能力。2. 架构影响微乎其微：参数量更大的Transformer在域内性能与单阶段检测器无统计学差异，且在CPU上速度慢5.5倍，不支持标准ONNX运行。3. 特定干预措施失败：LoRA编码器表现不佳，基础分割器导致掩膜质量下降39%，解剖标签优化仅带来微小增益（+0.0007）。4. 零样本迁移中mask mAP50-95损失62%，误差主要集中在牙根尖三分之一区域。

Conclusion: 边界精度是限制自动牙齿分割性能的瓶颈因素。未来的研究重点应从追求新颖的网络架构转向提高图像分辨率和采集数据的多样性，以改善边界识别能力。

Abstract: Automatic tooth segmentation and FDI numbering on panoramic radiographs underpins computer-assisted dental diagnosis, yet which factors govern performance remains unclear. We assemble a corpus of 1,422 panoramic radiographs containing 42,142 expert-delineated tooth polygons across the 32-class FDI taxonomy, annotated by 30 dental practitioners and independently reviewed by two others, and use it to isolate input resolution, architecture and anatomical priors under a single evaluation protocol.
  First, resolution dominates: across a controlled 640/1024/1280 ablation, mask mAP50-95 rises 0.656 -> 0.710 -> 0.717 while mAP50 stays flat at ~0.982. Both gains are significant under a paired bootstrap over images (p < 0.001, p = 0.024); neither mAP50 change is distinguishable from zero. Added resolution buys boundary precision, not detection. Second, architecture is nearly irrelevant in-domain: a query-based transformer with 2.1x the parameters is statistically equivalent to a one-stage detector (95% CI [-0.0064, +0.0064]), only marginally better under domain shift, 5.5x slower on CPU and not executable under standard ONNX runtimes. Third, three targeted interventions fail: a LoRA-adapted self-supervised encoder underperforms, a promptable foundation segmenter degrades masks by 39%, and globally optimal anatomical label assignment yields +0.0007 despite correcting a constraint violated in 40% of out-of-domain predictions.
  Zero-shot transfer to an independent multi-centre cohort, verified overlap-free, costs 62% of mask mAP50-95 but only 18% of mAP50, reproducing the dissociation. Decomposing masks along the tooth axis localises the residual error to the apical third. Boundary precision is therefore the binding constraint, and effort is better directed at resolution and acquisition diversity than at architectural novelty.

</details>


### [51] [Extending Decoupled Attention to Dense Prediction and Masked Training for Multi-Channel Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21629)
*Umar Marikkar,Sameed Husain,Muhammad Awais,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DC-ViT，通过解耦通道内和跨通道更新解决MC-ViT特征稀释问题，支持独立掩码训练并在多类成像基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道成像（MCI）数据中各通道记录语义不同的信号，传统MC-ViT将所有通道token拼接后计算自注意力，导致单个通道特征被稀释；且现有方法难以处理通道间可见token不一致的情况。

Method: 1. 将更新分为通道内更新和跨通道更新，先形成单通道表示再合并；2. 通过线性分配解决独立通道掩码下的token对应关系，确保每通道保留的patch能正确配对；3. 兼容标准配置的掩码多通道训练。

Result: 在荧光显微镜、成像质谱细胞术和卫星成像的6个分类与分割基准（含高密度通道预测任务）上，该方法性能超过最强的MC-ViT基线模型。

Conclusion: DC-ViT通过解耦注意力机制和灵活的token对应策略，有效适配MCI数据特性，提升了视觉编码器在多通道成像任务中的表现。

Abstract: Multi-Channel imaging (MCI) data differs fundamentally from natural images, as each channel records a semantically distinct signal rather than a colour band. To adapt vision encoders to MCI data, Multi-Channel Vision Transformers (MC-ViTs) tokenize each channel independently and concatenate the resulting tokens into one sequence, and the channel count is no longer fixed by the architecture. Self-attention is then computed across all channel-patch tokens with no restriction on which channels attend to which, which dilutes the features of individual channels. The Decoupled Vision Transformer (DC-ViT) regulates this by separating updates computed within a channel from updates computed across channels, and by forming a representation per channel before the channels are combined. Its formulation, however, pairs tokens by spatial position, and thus requires the same visible tokens in every channel. Correspondence under independent per-channel masking is recovered by solving a linear assignment between the retained patches of each channel, which allows decoupled attention to be combined with current masked multi-channel training in its standard configuration rather than a restricted one. Across three classification and three segmentation benchmarks spanning fluorescence microscopy, imaging mass cytometry and satellite imaging, including dense prediction at high channel counts, the resulting formulation outperforms the strongest MC-ViT baseline.

</details>


### [52] [Configurable Multi-Stage Vision Pipeline for Crop Disease and Pest Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21651)
*Naga Ganesh,Chandrashekar M S,Lakshmi Pedapudi,Aakash Singh,Vineet Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对Farmer.Chat服务中图像质量差、缺乏文本输入及现有系统不可调的问题，研究基于116万张照片的数据，对比了单一大模型（Qwen3-VL-4B）与分层专家模型（DaViT, YOLO26等）在图像质量筛选、作物识别和病虫害诊断上的表现。结果显示分层模型在精度上更优且无拒答现象，而大模型具备全流程单次调用及请求重拍图像的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Farmer.Chat生产系统无法调整阈值，不能添加新的作物或问题类型，且缺乏置信度截止设置。面对由廉价手机在田间弱光环境下拍摄的低质量图像，系统亟需一个可调节、高精度且能处理复杂场景的解决方案。

Method: 研究分析了来自埃塞俄比亚、印度、肯尼亚和尼日利亚的约116万张图像，将任务分为三个阶段：质量门控（M0）、作物检测（M1）和疾病/害虫检测（M2）。提出了两种路线进行对比：Route A使用微调后的视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL-4B）通过单次调用完成所有阶段；Route B使用小型专用模型（如DaViT, YOLO26）分别填充各阶段。同时用MobileNetV3替换原有的GPT-4o质量门控以提升效率。

Result: 1. 数据洞察：生产质量门控拒绝了46.8%的图像，超过四分之一的诊断返回无作物名称，且35.8%标记为“疾病”的实际是无需作物信息即可识别的害虫。
2. 性能对比：分层架构中的DaViT-Base在作物识别准确率达到95.41%，优于生产基线的91.46%。
3. 诊断表现：分层模型在诊断任务上领先，且从不拒绝回答；相比之下，所有参与比较的语言模型均有大量数据行未给出诊断结果。
4. 效率优化：MobileNetV3作为质量门控实现了86.9%的F1分数，耗时仅12毫秒。

Conclusion: 分层专家模型（Route B）在作物识别精度和诊断覆盖率上优于大语言模型方案，解决了LLM经常拒答的问题。然而，微调的大模型（Route A）保留了两个独特能力：一是通过单次调用完成全部三个阶段的处理，二是能在图像不支持回答时主动请求用户拍摄更好的照片，这提供了更大的灵活性和交互性。

Abstract: Farmer.Chat is Digital Green's farm advisory service for smallholder farmers. When something looks wrong with a crop, the farmer takes a photograph and sends it, and that photograph is the whole question: no symptom described, no crop named, often no text at all. The service has to determine whether the picture can be used, what crop it shows, and what is wrong with it, from images taken on cheap phones in a field, in poor light and with a moving camera. The system doing this today cannot be adjusted. It has no adjustable thresholds for photograph rejection, crops and problems cannot be added, and there is no confidence cut-off to set.
  We study about 1.16 million photographs sent to Farmer.Chat from Ethiopia, India, Kenya and Nigeria. The production quality gate rejected 46.8% of the images it judged, over a quarter of those reaching diagnosis returned no crop name, and 35.8% of the labelled problems filed under "disease" are pests, identifiable without the crop. We therefore split the work into three stages: a quality gate (M0), a crop detector (M1), and a disease or pest detector (M2). Route A fills all three with one fine-tuned vision-language model (Qwen3-VL-4B) answering in a single call. Route B fills each with a small specialist model (DaViT, YOLO26).
  We replace our production GPT-4o quality gate with a small MobileNetV3 gate at 86.9% F1 in 12 ms. On one test set scored the same way for every system, a hierarchical DaViT-Base achieves 95.41% crop accuracy against 91.46% for the production baseline. It also leads on diagnosis and never declines to answer, while every language model in the comparison leaves a large share of rows with no diagnosis. The fine-tuned model retains two capabilities the specialists do not have: one call for all three stages, and a request for a better photograph when the image cannot support an answer.

</details>


### [53] [DRT: Dense Reasoning Trace for Efficient and Grounded Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21675)
*Wan Xu,Yuanfan Guo,Kevin Han,LaLa Chen,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dense Reasoning Trace (DRT)范式，通过结构化紧凑推理轨迹替代自然语言CoT，显著提升Token效率并增强视觉 grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有链式思维（CoT）范式依赖自然语言表达，导致语言学开销过大、信息稀释及视觉 grounding 能力薄弱。

Method: 1. Dense Trace Initialization：将DRT推理模式内化至模型，平衡Token效率与视觉证据保留；2. Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning：构建参考轨迹并通过Tri-perspective verification pipeline验证，采用Trace-Grounded GRPO与结构化奖励，鼓励生成简洁且逻辑严谨的DRT轨迹。

Result: 在具有挑战性的推理基准测试中，DRT相比Qwen3-VL基线实现了5.5倍的Token效率提升，准确率提高1.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 复杂的 multimodal 推理无需冗长的自然语言轨迹，DRT为下一代MLLMs提供了一条更高效的路径。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), prevailing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigms remain confined to the natural-language expression space. Consequently, they inherently incur excessive linguistic overhead, leading to information dilution and weak visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose Dense Reasoning Trace (DRT), a paradigm that departs from natural-language-centered CoT by expressing reasoning as compact structured traces, which include concise intermediate states with symbolic connectors and disentangle visual observations from logical deductions. First, we introduce the Dense Trace Initialization to internalize the DRT reasoning mode into the model, substantially improving token efficiency while preserving visual evidence. To further enable the model to faithfully capture the logical relations within traces, we propose the Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning framework, which builds reference traces through a tri-perspective verification pipeline and employs Trace-Grounded GRPO with structured rewards, encouraging the model to generate concise DRT-style traces with reduced hallucination and stronger logical grounding. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning benchmarks show that DRT achieves 5.5$\times$ token efficiency improvement while improving 1.3 accuracy points over the Qwen3-VL baseline. These findings suggest that complex multimodal reasoning may not require verbose natural-language traces, opening a more efficient path for next-generation MLLMs. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/HIT-leaderone/DRT

</details>


### [54] [Diffusion-Based Tumor Inpainting for Renal Segmentation under Clinical Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21698)
*Ekaterina Sedykh,Salme Ussanov,Dmytro Fedorenko,Dmytro Fishman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的肾脏肿瘤合成框架，通过2.5D和3D增强策略在低数据场景下显著降低假阳性率，且2.5D方法在保持性能的同时计算成本低于全3D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习肾脏肿瘤分割需要大量标注数据，但临床部署通常仅有少量目标部位肿瘤阳性病例，缺乏足够的数据支持模型训练。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的修复（inpainting）框架，在健康CT扫描中合成解剖学合理的肾脏肿瘤，无需额外标注；系统比较2D、2.5D和全3D（MAISI）合成策略，使用公共数据（KiTS23, KIRC）训练扩散模型，并在内部队列的三种低数据模式下评估nnU-Net分割性能。

Result: 2.5D和3D增强显著将假阳性率从~18-20%降低至~3-6%，同时维持Dice系数；2D方法无一致益处；2.5D方法在所有指标上匹配全3D合成，但计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 局部体积一致性足以在数据和资源匮乏的临床环境中实现有效的数据增强，2.5D方法是平衡性能与效率的优选方案。

Abstract: Deep learning segmentation of renal tumors requires large annotated datasets, yet clinical deployments typically offer only a handful of tumor-positive cases from the target site. We propose a diffusion-based inpainting framework that synthesizes anatomically plausible renal tumors within healthy CT scans, requiring no additional annotation, and provide the first systematic comparison of 2D, 2.5D, and full 3D (MAISI) synthesis strategies for this task. Training the diffusion model on public data (KiTS23, KIRC) and evaluating nnU-Net segmentation on a internal cohort across three low-data regimes, we find that 2.5D and 3D augmentation substantially reduce false positives (from $\sim$18--20\% to $\sim$3--6\%) while maintaining Dice, whereas 2D provides no consistent benefit. Crucially, the proposed 2.5D method matches full 3D synthesis on every metric at substantially lower computational cost, indicating that local volumetric consistency alone is sufficient for effective augmentation in data- and resource-scarce clinical settings.

</details>


### [55] [SignGPT: Toward LLM-Mediated Sign Language Interaction through Gloss-Free Translation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21709)
*Ronghui Li,Jun Dong,Zhongyuan Hu,Zunnan Xu,Jun Zhou,Liyuan Chen,Shuoling Liu,Jiangpeng Yan,Jie Guo,Xiu Li,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SignGPT，一个统一的、基于姿态的框架，用于无词表的签名语言翻译(SLT)和生成(SLG)，支持双向建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型(LLMs)对签名语言交互支持有限；分别使用SLT和SLG模型会导致在签名-文本交互中频繁切换模型，效率低下。统一两种任务可实现签名作为输入和输出的无缝交互。

Method: 构建SignGPT框架，将身体、手部和面部运动的局部感知分层表示整合到共享语言模型中；采用非对称多token预测和渐进式训练实现双向建模。

Result: 在How2Sign (ASL) 和 Phoenix-2014T (DGS) 数据集上通过基准测试、定性分析和组件消融实验验证了有效性；12名聋人ASL签署者的探索性研究表明，LLM介导的“签名到签名”响应管道具有潜力，支持签名语言对话(SLC)。

Conclusion: 统一的签名语言建模方法能有效支持更自然的签名语言交互与对话，为LLM在签名语言领域的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide limited support for sign language interaction. Unifying sign language translation (SLT) and generation (SLG) to enable sign language as both input and output can reduce switching between separate models during sign-text interaction. We present SignGPT, a unified, pose-based framework for gloss-free SLT and SLG. SignGPT integrates part-aware hierarchical representations of body, hand, and facial motion into a shared language model and employs asymmetric multi-token prediction and progressive training for bidirectional modeling. We evaluate SignGPT on How2Sign (ASL) and Phoenix-2014T (DGS) through benchmark comparisons, qualitative analyses, and component ablations. An exploratory study with 12 Deaf ASL signers assesses an LLM-mediated sign-to-sign response pipeline, highlighting the potential of unified modeling to support sign language conversation (SLC).

</details>


### [56] [ZYT-World: A Real-Time Controllable World Model for Closed-Loop Autonomous-Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21712)
*Boni Hu,Xiong Wei,Haoming Huang,Yong Huang,Chenbo Wang,Yi Yang,Jiancheng Wang,Ruicheng Zhu,Zhimin Yang,Guanglai Liu,Qiaowan Jin,Dongzhuo Wang,Haiwei Kuang,Jiajun Fan,Yue Wu,Jiaxin Wei,Hao Sun,Feihong Yan,Wei Bi,Kaixuan Wang,Zichao Guo,Xiaozhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ZYT-World，一种单架构生成式世界模型，原生支持4视场鱼眼和3视场针孔相机。通过投影特定Plucker适配器、自回归蒸馏和TinyVAE解码器，实现从40步双向教师到单步流式生成的转化，在保持高保真度的同时推理速度提升107.7倍，并具备场景记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生产部署中三个未决需求：1) 以原生分辨率忠实复现混合鱼眼-针孔相机 rig；2) 平衡逐时间步交互的因果性与长视界稳定性及低延迟；3) 在重访地点时保留场景身份（长期一致性）。

Method: 1) **架构设计**：使用投影特定Plucker适配器编码相机几何，自运动自适应层归一化提供全局运动控制，轻量级像素对齐布局条件化交通参与者。2) **训练策略**：异构训练结合全rig几何覆盖与高分辨率细节；采用教师强制、因果一致性蒸馏、自 rollout 分布匹配蒸馏将40步双向教师转化为单步生成器；引入RigCritic联合评估七视图 rig。3) **优化加速**：使用19M参数的TinyVAE解码器、W8A8量化及专用推理引擎降低计算成本。4) **记忆机制**：基于真实采集数据训练隐式记忆模块以保留位置特异性证据。

Result: 1) **性能指标**：单步模型保留教师模型90%以上的PSNR和SSIM，FID、FVD和LPIPS保持在教师模型的11%误差范围内。2) **效率提升**：相比40步双向教师，推理速度快107.7倍；TinyVAE解码速度比Wan快59.8倍。3) **功能验证**：30秒 rollout 和跨轨迹重访实验验证了长视界稳定性和场景记忆行为的有效性。

Conclusion: ZYT-World 通过单一架构实现了多视角、高分辨率、低延迟且具备长期一致性的生成式世界模型，成功解决了端到端驾驶策略仿真中的保真度、实时性和记忆性难题，为生产部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Generative world models offer controllable and repeatable closed-loop simulation for end-to-end and vision-language-action driving policies, but production deployment exposes three unresolved requirements: faithfully reproducing a mixed fisheye-pinhole rig at native resolutions; reconciling causal, per-timestep interaction with long-horizon stability and low latency; and preserving scene identity when a location is revisited. We present ZYT-World, a single architecture that natively generates four fisheye views with field of view > 180° and three pinhole views. Projection-specific Plucker adapters encode camera geometry, ego-motion adaptive layer normalization provides global motion control, and a lightweight pixel-aligned layout conditions traffic participants and signals through instance-level boxes, headings and colors. Heterogeneous training combines full-rig geometric coverage with high-resolution detail. Teacher forcing, causal consistency distillation, self-rollout distribution matching distillation, and RigCritic transform a 40-step bidirectional teacher into a one-step, per-latent streaming generator, with RigCritic evaluating the seven-view rig jointly. A 19M-parameter variational autoencoder decoder (TinyVAE), W8A8 quantization, and our inference engine reduce decoding, backbone, and incremental-execution costs, respectively. Finally, cross-trajectory pairs derived from real captures train a plug-in implicit-memory module that preserves place-specific evidence. On the internal multi-view test set, the one-step model retains more than 90% of the teacher's PSNR and SSIM, while FID, FVD, and LPIPS stay within 11% of the teacher. Under the generator-only timing in Figure 2, it is 107.7 times faster than the 40-step bidirectional teacher. TinyVAE decodes 59.8 times faster than Wan. 30s rollouts and cross-trajectory revisits show the intended long-horizon and memory behavior.

</details>


### [57] [Balanced Prompt Adaptation against Entropy-Induced Collapse for Test-Time Binary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21743)
*Zhengshan Wang,Joshua Charles Webster-Ford,Yifei Tian,Xinxin Wang,Long Chen,Weiping Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BAPA方法，通过平衡锚点选择和动态提示适应解决测试时二值分割中熵最小化导致的类别不平衡崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统熵最小化在测试时适应（TTA）的二值分割任务中，因像素预测聚合导致大类别主导更新，引发少数类预测坍塌和掩膜退化。

Method: 提出平衡锚点提示适应（BAPA），包含两类平衡锚点（CBA）模块以均衡前景/背景损失权重，及动态提示适应（DPA）模块仅优化文本侧提示残差以保持视觉-语言编码器冻结。

Result: 在四个域的实验表明，BAPA取得了最高的平均Dice系数；因子分解消融实验验证了CBA和DPA的互补作用。

Conclusion: 平衡提示适应是测试时二值分割中替代熵最小化的有效方法，能防止模型坍塌并提升性能。

Abstract: Entropy minimization is a standard objective for test-time adaptation (TTA), but it can fail in imbalanced binary segmentation. Unlike image classification, dense segmentation aggregates thousands of pixel predictions, allowing the larger predicted class to dominate the update, pull minority predictions toward itself, and produce a degenerate mask as predictions saturate and their entropy gradients vanish. We theoretically establish this collapse in a shared-shift model. This analysis motivates Balanced-Anchor Prompt Adaptation (BAPA), which combines two complementary modules. The Class-Balanced Anchors (CBA) module selects high-confidence anchors separately from each predicted class and gives foreground and background equal total loss weight, preventing the larger region from dominating the update. Dynamic Prompt Adaptation (DPA) refreshes these anchors after each prediction update and optimizes only text-side prompt residuals while keeping the vision-language encoders frozen. This prompt-only update refines the foreground-background decision boundary without altering the pretrained dense visual representation. Across experiments from four domains, BAPA achieves the highest mean Dice among the evaluated methods. Factorized ablations further validate the complementary roles of CBA and DPA, supporting balanced prompt adaptation as an effective alternative to entropy minimization for test-time binary segmentation.

</details>


### [58] [SFVO: Decoupled Confidence-Guided Stereo-Flow Visual Odometry with Bidirectional PnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21754)
*Kai Zhang,Guoyang Zhao,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SFVO，一种基于预训练立体匹配和光流模型的对应关系驱动的立体视觉里程计框架，通过几何约束和解耦置信度图实现鲁棒、准确的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度视觉里程计存在尺度模糊问题；立体视觉虽能提供真实度量信息，但因计算成本高和建模复杂而研究较少；尽管立体匹配和光流技术已成熟，但其互补的几何信息尚未在视觉里程计中得到充分利用。

Method: 构建SFVO框架，直接利用预训练的立体匹配和光流模型估计立体和时间对应关系；将学习到的对应关系映射为几何约束并预测可信点；引入针对旋转和平移的解耦置信度图，以更好地对齐视觉对应关系与6自由度变换的特性。

Result: 在室外和室内数据集上的广泛实验表明，SFVO实现了鲁棒且准确的位姿估计，并具有强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SFVO有效利用了预训练模型的互补几何信息，解决了立体视觉里程计的建模难题，提供了高精度且泛化性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning-based visual odometry (VO) has achieved significant progress, yet most existing methods focus on a monocular approach, which suffers from scale ambiguity. Stereo VO provides real metric by its nature, but remains less studied in deep learning VO due to its high computational cost and modeling complexity. Recent advances in stereo matching and optical flow estimation have made dense visual correspondence increasingly accurate and reliable, but their complementary geometric information has not been fully exploited for VO. In this paper, we present SFVO, a correspondence-driven stereo VO framework that directly builds upon pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models. SFVO exploits pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models to estimate stereo and temporal correspondences. Instead of learning pose directly from images, SFVO maps learned correspondences into geometric constraints and predicts which points are trustworthy. To improve the reliability of visual correspondence-based geometric constraints, we introduce decoupled confidence maps for rotation and translation. This design better aligns the characteristics of visual correspondence and 6-DoF transformations. Extensive experiments on outdoor and indoor datasets demonstrate that SFVO achieves robust and accurate pose estimation with strong generalization capability. The code will be released.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Benchmark Scores: Auditing Medical Vision-Language Models for Chest X-Ray Tuberculosis Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21763)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究审计了三个医学视觉-语言模型在胸部X光结核筛查任务中的表现，发现模型排名、分数可靠性和筛选性能在不同评估设置（队列、提示词、阴性谱等）下显著变化，证明单一基准分数不能保证临床可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证关于模型排名、分数可靠性和筛选性能的声明是否在不同的评估设置（如队列、提示词、阴性对照、指定患病率和操作阈值）下依然成立，以评估模型的临床可移植性。

Method: 对BioMedCLIP、CheXficient、MedSigLIP和OpenCLIP四个模型进行审计，使用12,200张来自四个数据集的胸部X光图像。通过五类固定提示词家族生成244,000个模型-图像-提示词得分，分析AUROC变化、不同阴性对照的影响、阈值保留能力及外部验证表现。

Result: 1. 没有模型在所有队列和可靠性标准中均领先；提示词变化导致21/48比较中AUROC显著改变。2. 用非结核病人替换健康对照使AUROC下降0.075–0.306。3. 医学模型在无病变对照上表现优于肺炎或肿瘤对照，部分疾病AUROC低于0.5。4. CheXficient存在数据泄露风险。5. 高内部验证AUROC（0.999）与低外部验证AUROC（0.629）之间存在巨大差距，保守排除无法完全弥合。

Conclusion: 区分能力、分数可靠性和阈值保留支持不同的可移植性主张。证据表明，胸部X光结核筛查的证据应基于完整评估规范，而非仅依赖检查点（checkpoint），单一基准分数不足以确立临床适用性。

Abstract: A medical model's benchmark score does not establish that the same conclusion holds under a different evaluation. This study tests whether claims about model ranking, score reliability and screening performance survive changes in cohort, prompt, negative spectrum, specified prevalence and operating threshold. We audit three medical vision-language models (BioMedCLIP, CheXficient, and MedSigLIP) and a general-domain OpenCLIP comparator on 12,200 chest radiograph records from four datasets (Montgomery, Shenzhen, TBX11K, and VinDr-CXR). Five fixed prompt families yield 244,000 model--image--prompt scores. No model leads every cohort and reliability criterion. Prompt-family changes alter AUROC in 21 of 48 multiplicity-controlled comparisons. Replacing healthy controls with sick non-tuberculosis controls reduces AUROC by 0.075--0.306 across all four models. On VinDr-CXR, the three medical models distinguish tuberculosis from no-finding controls substantially better than from pneumonia or lung tumor; their AUROC point estimates for both named diseases fall below 0.5. CheXficient has documented VinDr-CXR pretraining exposure, which limits the interpretation of its results. Thresholds chosen for 95\% sensitivity on TBX11K training retain that constraint by point estimate in only four of sixteen target evaluations. A five-seed supervised source model reaches 0.999 AUROC on TBX11K validation but 0.629 on each of two external cohorts. Conservative exclusion of perceptual-overlap candidates narrows this gap without closing it. These retrospective, single-task results show that discrimination, score reliability and threshold retention support different portability claims. Evidence for chest X-ray tuberculosis screening should identify the complete evaluation specification rather than attribute clinical portability to a checkpoint alone.

</details>


### [60] [XCalib Depth-Guided Geometric Optimization for Dense Thermal-Visible Video Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21770)
*Aurelien Godet,Gabriel Jobert,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出XCalib，一种无监督的热红外-可见光密集配准框架，通过虚拟针孔相机参数化作为几何约束，优化相对位姿、内参和单目深度，解决ADAS中因光轴不重合导致的非均匀视差和视觉重影问题，提升时间稳定性和对齐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）的多模态感知任务中，可见光（RGB）与红外（IR）相机因光轴不重合和视场角差异产生空间错位，导致非均匀视差和视觉重影。传统关键点方法受限于全局仿射变换，无法处理动态深度；而无约束密集光流算法缺乏结构正则化且存在时间不稳定问题。

Method: 提出XCalib框架，将虚拟针孔相机参数化严格作为几何约束空间而非绝对度量校准工具。通过联合优化有效相对位姿、相机内参以及预测的单目度量深度，将空间位移的搜索限制在物理上有效的投影几何范围内。核心组件包括：1) 利用相机参数化作为密集跨模态扭曲的隐式正则器的新型配准范式；2) 归一化边缘相关性（NEC），一种专为跨光谱对齐设计的鲁棒结构相似性指标。

Result: 在公共ADAS数据集上进行了广泛的定量和定性评估。结果表明，XCalib在无约束密集光流基线方法之上，显著提升了时间稳定性和对齐精度。

Conclusion: XCalib通过引入几何约束和结构相似性度量，有效解决了多模态图像配准中的动态深度和非均匀视差挑战，为ADAS提供了更稳定、准确的预处理方案。

Abstract: Image registration is a vital preprocessing step in multimodal perception tasks, including image fusion, object detection, and semantic segmentation. In Advanced Driver- Assistance Systems (ADAS), spatial misalignment between visible (RGB) and infrared (IR) cameras -caused by non-coincident optical axes and field-of-view differences- introduces non-uniform parallax and visual ghosting. Classical keypoint-based methods are restricted to global homographies that fail under dynamic depth, while unconstrained dense flow algorithms lack structural regularization and suffer from temporal instability. In this paper, we propose XCalib, an unsupervised dense thermal-visible registration framework that bridges this gap. Rather than serving as an absolute metric calibration tool, XCalib leverages virtual pinhole camera parameterization strictly as a geometric constraint space. By optimizing effective relative pose and intrinsics alongside predicted monocular metric depth, XCalib restricts the search space of spatial displacements to physically valid projection geometries. Our key contributions are: (1) a novel registration paradigm that uses camera parameterization as an implicit regularizer for dense cross-modal warping; (2) Normalized Edges Correlation (NEC), a robust structural similarity metric tailored to cross- spectral alignment; and (3) extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations across public ADAS datasets, demonstrating superior temporal stability and alignment accuracy over unconstrained dense flow baselines.

</details>


### [61] [PointLAM: Local Attentive Mamba for Efficient Point-based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21780)
*Xuanming Shang,Weijia Zhang,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PointLAM，一种高效点基3D目标检测架构。通过Laplacian Point Sampler (LPS) 加速下采样，利用Local Hadamard Aggregator (LHA) 和Bi-Directional Mamba (BDM) 优化局部交互与全局建模，在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上实现高性能且低计算开销，特别擅长小目标和极端稀疏场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决点基方法中存在的系统性效率低下问题：传统的FPS下采样策略缓慢，k-NN动态邻居查询及连续特征交互计算成本高昂，导致严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 1. **Laplacian Point Sampler (LPS)**: 采用隐式离散拉普拉斯高通滤波器和双重排序采样技术，实现快速、感知结构的前景保留以解决下采样瓶颈。
2. **Local Hadamard Aggregator (LHA)**: 使用瞬态网格将空间索引与特征表示解耦，并用Hadamard门控机制替代复杂的连续交互，实现拓扑感知的注意力调制。
3. **Local Attentive Mamba (LAM) Block**: 结合LHA局部门控与Bi-Directional Mamba (BDM) 层进行全局序列建模。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上，PointLAM作为点基检测器取得了具有竞争力的性能，媲美高度优化的体素竞争对手，同时显著降低计算 footprint。在小实例检测和处理极端稀疏性方面表现出明显优势。

Conclusion: PointLAM通过创新的采样和聚合机制，成功克服了点基架构的计算瓶颈，证明了其在保持几何保真度的同时实现高效率的可行性，为3D目标检测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds faces a fundamental dilemma: voxel-based methods achieve efficiency at the cost of geometric quantization, while point-based methods preserve fidelity but suffer from prohibitive computational bottlenecks. Specifically, point-based architectures are crippled by slow downsampling strategies (e.g., FPS) and expensive dynamic neighbor queries (e.g., k-NN) coupled with costly continuous interactions. To tackle these systemic inefficiencies, we propose PointLAM, a highly efficient and powerful point-based architecture driven by two synergistic innovations. First, to resolve the downsampling bottleneck, we develop the Laplacian Point Sampler (LPS). LPS employs an implicit discrete Laplacian high-pass filter and Doubly Sorted Sampling to achieve fast, structure-aware foreground preservation. Second, to overcome local modeling latency, we design the Local Hadamard Aggregator (LHA). LHA decouples spatial indexing from feature representation using transient grids, and replaces complex continuous interactions with a Hadamard Gating mechanism for topology-aware, attentive modulation. By coupling this local gating with Bi-Directional Mamba (BDM) layers for global sequence modeling, we formulate the Local Attentive Mamba (LAM) block. Powered by this architecture, PointLAM achieves competitive performance on nuScenes and Waymo for point-based detectors. It rivals highly optimized voxel competitors while requiring a fraction of the computational footprint, demonstrating marked superiority in detecting small instances and handling extreme sparsity. Project page: https://pointlam.github.io/.

</details>


### [62] [A Principled Approach to Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21800)
*James Myles,Matthew Baugh,Johanna P. Müller,Bernhard Kainz,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将无监督异常检测（UAD）重构为贝叶斯逆问题，通过推断导致观测值的潜在“损坏”参数来生成概率异常分数。该方法不仅提升MVTec AD数据集上的检测性能（AUROC提高2.3%），还能在脑MRI数据中估计病理强度、偏差和几何特征，兼顾了异常的存在性与本质属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无监督异常检测方法仅关注偏离规范分布的标记或定位，忽略了异常的底层生成机制。然而，异常的性质往往与其存在同样重要。现有的方法缺乏对异常产生原因的概率化推断能力。

Method: 将UAD形式化为贝叶斯逆问题，目标是为每个观测值推断最可能的“损坏”（corruption）。该框架以推断出的损坏参数的能量作为概率异常评分标准，提供了一个开发新UAD算法的原则性食谱。通过不同的建模选择，可以将多种现有方法推导为该通用框架的特例。

Result: 1. 在控制环境下验证了框架各组件的有效性；2. 通过调整底层损坏模型，在MVTec AD数据集上将对象类别AUROC提升了2.3%；3. 在脑MRI基准测试中实现了强大的检测性能，并能同时输出病理强度、偏差和几何形状的估计值。

Conclusion: 提出的贝叶斯逆问题框架为UAD提供了统一且 principled 的理论基础，不仅能提高检测精度，还能提供关于异常性质的丰富解释性信息。代码已开源。

Abstract: Traditional unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) methods are designed to flag or localise deviations from a normative distribution, ignoring the underlying generative mechanisms of the anomalies. Yet the nature of an anomaly is often as important as its presence. We reformulate UAD as a Bayesian inverse problem, in which the objective is to infer the most probable corruption responsible for each observation. Our framework yields a probabilistic anomaly score as the energy of the inferred corruption parameters, and serves as a principled recipe for developing new UAD algorithms. We derive several existing methods as instances of the general framework, each corresponding to the same energy score under different modelling choices. Experimentally, we study the framework's components in a controlled setting, and improve object-class AUROC on the MVTec AD dataset by 2.3% by adapting the underlying corruption model. Finally, we validate the framework on a brain MRI benchmark, achieving strong detection performance while producing estimates of pathology intensity, bias, and geometry. Code is available at https://github.com/jgmyles/inverse-uad.

</details>


### [63] [VideoReloc: Long-Term Indoor Video Relocalization against a Kilobyte-Scale Semantic Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21804)
*Qianru Li,Xuyang Chen,Xuqin Wang,Zhenghao Zhang,Hongyi Luo,Tao Wu,Daniel Cremers,Lu Liu,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoReloc，一种基于紧凑语义场景图的长时序室内视频重定位方法。通过自适应视频片段收集时空证据，结合假设优先注册和方向感知细化，在光照和家具变化下实现高精度位姿估计，仅需100 kB地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉重定位方法依赖外观特征，在光照变化和家具变动下不可靠；而基于单帧对象类别和几何的局部化方法证据稀疏且存在歧义。需要一种能利用时序上下文、适应动态环境且地图轻量化的鲁棒重定位方案。

Method: 引入VideoReloc框架：1) 使用里程计构建自适应视频片段，根据对象和运动标准动态调整查询长度以积累空间证据；2) 运行级决策通过连接片段的累积证据重新检查冲突位置，稳定轨迹；3) 假设优先注册利用对象三元组生成姿态假设，并通过片段级对象中心和边界框表面进行验证；4) 方向感知细化利用边界框面、重力和墙壁方向解决相机朝向歧义并精细化全姿态。

Result: 在RIO10和ReplicaCAD数据集上，因果评估下1m/10°的全帧定位成功率分别为73.5%和61.1%，加入片段闭合后提升至90.6%和74.8%。相比之下，现有的逐帧场景坐标回归器成功率仅为47.6%-49.8%，且需要12.6-42 MB的大型地图。

Conclusion: 该方法将稀疏地图重定位重构为对空间扩展视频查询的验证过程，将判别支持从存储的外观转移到时序上下文，实现了在极小地图（100 kB）规模下的高精度、鲁棒性长时序室内视频重定位。

Abstract: Given a compact semantic scene graph, long-term indoor video relocalization estimates a map-frame trajectory after lighting and furniture changes. Visual methods rely on appearance and become unreliable under these changes; localizing one frame at a time from object classes and geometry instead leaves sparse, ambiguous evidence. We introduce VideoReloc, whose adaptive clips use odometry to gather spatial evidence until object and motion criteria are met, adapting query length to the observed scene. Its run-level decision rechecks conflicting placements using evidence accumulated across connected clips, stabilizing the trajectory beyond adjacent-clip tracking. Hypothesis-first registration proposes poses from object triplets and verifies each using clip-wide object centers and box surfaces. Orientation-aware refinement uses box faces, gravity and wall directions to resolve ambiguity in camera orientation and refine the full pose. This reframes sparse-map relocalization as verification of spatially extended video queries, moving discriminative support from stored appearance to temporal context and permitting a 100 kB map of class-labelled boxes. On RIO10 and ReplicaCAD, the all-frame localization success rate at 1 m/10$^\circ$ is 73.5% and 61.1% under causal evaluation, rising to 90.6% and 74.8% with clip closure. The evaluated per-frame scene coordinate regressors reach up to 47.6% and 49.8%, respectively, with maps of 12.6-42 MB. Project page: https://videoreloc.github.io

</details>


### [64] [Object Detection Benchmarks are Incomplete: The Role of Label Errors and Annotation Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21822)
*Sarina Penquitt,Jonathan Klees,Antonia van Betteray,Parssa Jashnieh,Peter Stehr,Matthias Rottmann,Lars Schmarje*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出当前目标检测基准的质量受限于标注不完整，通过重新标注发现大量遗漏实例。为此提出了高召回率、不确定性感知的标注管道及两个新基准（不确定性感知检测和标签错误检测），揭示了现有模型对标注质量的依赖及其与人类感知的错位，呼吁未来基准向高召回率和不确定性评估转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管目标检测架构和开放词汇模型不断进步，但基准测试质量受到标注不完整性的严重限制，导致模型评估无法真实反映其在现实世界模糊场景下的性能。

Method: 1. 在COCO、Pascal VOC、Cityscapes和KITTI四个数据集上进行重新标注，量化标注缺失情况；2. 开发可扩展的标注管道，强调高召回率，并通过至少11名 annotators 聚合软标签以捕捉歧义；3. 构建两个大规模基准：不确定性感知目标检测基准和基于真实标签错误的标签错误检测基准。

Result: 1. 重新标注显示 KITTI 和 COCO 的数据量分别增加高达60%和40%，主要源于之前未标注的小尺寸、遮挡或密集实例；2. 缺失标注是所有数据集中标签错误的主要来源；3. 新标注提高了覆盖率并与人类校准良好对齐；4. 基准性能对标注质量高度敏感，但模型排名基本稳定；5. 当前检测器严重依赖标注质量且与人类感知不一致；6. 现有的标签错误检测方法在真实标签错误上难以实现高召回率和精度。

Conclusion: 未来的目标检测基准应超越确定性标注，转向高召回率、不确定性感知的评估体系，以最大化有效实例并更好地反映现实世界的模糊性。

Abstract: While object detection has advanced through improved architectures and open-vocabulary models, we provide strong evidence that benchmark quality is limited by annotation incompleteness. Across four widely used datasets (COCO, Pascal VOC, Cityscapes, KITTI), re-annotation reveals substantial increases in annotated objects (e.g., up to +60% on KITTI and +40% on COCO), driven primarily by previously unlabeled small, occluded, or densely packed instances. While some differences arise from dataset-specific annotation conventions, we consistently find that missing annotations are the main source of label errors across all datasets. To achieve high data quality, we introduce a scalable annotation pipeline that emphasizes high recall and captures ambiguity through soft labels aggregated from at least 11 annotators per object. The resulting annotations improve coverage and align well with human calibration. We show that benchmark performance is highly sensitive to annotation quality, although model rankings remain largely stable. We introduce two large-scale benchmarks: (i) an uncertainty-aware object detection benchmark, and (ii) a label error detection benchmark grounded in real label errors. We show that current detectors are strongly depended on annotation quality and are misaligned with human perception. Current label error detection methods, which have been shown to perform well on synthetic noise, struggle to achieve high recall and precision on real label errors. Our results highlight the need for future object detection benchmarks to move beyond deterministic annotations toward high-recall, uncertainty-aware evaluation that maximizes valid instances and better reflects real-world ambiguity.

</details>


### [65] [Morphology-Aware Ambiguity Learning for Wafer Defect Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21866)
*Seungjun Chu,Seokhyun Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种形态感知的模糊学习框架，通过构建类别级模糊矩阵，在晶圆缺陷识别中实现自动单类诊断、辅助双类诊断和全面审查三种决策模式，优于传统固定分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶圆图缺陷识别通常被表述为固定分类问题，强制将每个晶圆分配给单一缺陷类别。然而，对于处于类别边界的晶圆，强制单一预测提供的信息量有限，无法提供合理的诊断备选方案。

Method: 利用训练晶圆图的径向、角度和几何特征，构建代表具有相似形态和合理诊断备选方案的缺陷类别对的类别级模糊矩阵。该矩阵指导模型学习合理的替代类别，而非平等对待所有错误类别。在推理阶段，根据不确定性判断是否提供有意义的两类别诊断集或升级为全面审查。

Result: 在WM-811K数据集上的实验表明，该框架在缺陷识别和诊断决策支持方面优于传统方法，能够提供有意义的两类别备选方案，并将全面审查保留给存在未解决歧义的情况。成本分析显示其路由策略具有潜在的成本优势，且诊断行为在不同骨干架构下保持一致。

Conclusion: 提出的形态感知模糊学习框架通过支持多粒度诊断行动（单类、双类备选、全面审查），有效解决了边界案例的识别难题，提升了缺陷识别的准确性和诊断决策的效率与经济性。

Abstract: Wafer map defect recognition is commonly formulated as a fixed-taxonomy classification problem that assigns each wafer to a single defect class. However, some wafers exhibit morphologies near class boundaries, for which forcing a single prediction may be less informative than providing plausible diagnostic alternatives. This paper proposes a morphology-aware ambiguity learning framework that supports three diagnostic actions: automatic single-class diagnosis, assisted diagnosis with two plausible defect classes, and full review. Using the radial, angular, and geometric characteristics of training wafer maps, the framework constructs a class-level ambiguity matrix representing defect-class pairs with similar morphology and plausible diagnostic alternatives. It guides the model to learn plausible alternative classes rather than treating all incorrect classes equally. During inference, the matrix determines whether an uncertain prediction can be represented by a meaningful two-class diagnostic set or should be escalated for full review. Experiments on WM-811K show that the proposed framework outperforms conventional approaches in defect recognition and diagnostic decision support, providing meaningful two-class alternatives while reserving full review for cases with unresolved ambiguity. Illustrative cost analyses further show the potential cost advantage of the proposed routing strategy. The diagnostic behavior of the framework remains consistent across different backbone architectures.

</details>


### [66] [Chronosphere: Space-Time Tessellation of Local Climate Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21872)
*Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Kexing Li,Brian Wei,Isaac Corley,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Chronosphere，一种结合自适应网格划分与局部基函数的时空神经网络场，用于学习气候表示，解决现有位置编码器在空间和时间细节建模上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理表示学习中存在的主要挑战是环境过程的空间和时间复杂性变化广泛，而现有的位置编码器通常固定单一细节级别，无法灵活适应不同区域和时间的复杂度需求。

Method: 将时空环面 $S^2\times S^1$ 上的可学习站点自适应网格划分与共享的局部基函数库相结合，使容量分布和细节程度随数据在空间和时间上自适应调整。

Result: 在重建气候学任务中，Chronosphere 在空间和时间任务上匹配或超越了最先进的状态（state-of-the-art）位置编码器，尤其在空间和时间的迁移任务中取得了最大提升。

Conclusion: Chronosphere 通过统一全局、局部和自适应方法，有效解决了地理表示学习中多尺度复杂性问题，提升了模型在时空任务及迁移学习中的性能。

Abstract: We introduce Chronosphere, a spatio-temporal neural field that learns representations of climate. A central challenge in geographic representation learning is modeling environmental processes whose spatial and temporal complexity varies widely. Yet existing location encoders typically fix a single level of detail everywhere. Global bases such as spherical harmonics spread capacity uniformly across space and time. Localized bases resolve only predefined regions. Learned tessellations adapt, but are inefficient at representing higher frequencies. Chronosphere unifies these approaches, pairing an adaptive tessellation of learnable sites on the spacetime torus $S^2\times S^1$ with a shared bank of local basis functions. Both where capacity is placed and how much detail each region carries adapt to the data, across space and time. Trained to reconstruct climatology, Chronosphere matches or leads state-of-the-art location encoders across spatial and temporal tasks, with the largest gains under spatial and temporal transfer.

</details>


### [67] [Benchmarking the Explanatory Quality of Open-Weight Vision-Language Models in Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21879)
*Laurent Colbois,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出针对视觉语言模型（VLMs）在面部识别中解释质量的基准测试框架，重点评估解释的相关性和忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM面部识别研究多关注识别准确率，而缺乏对生成解释有效性的量化评估，导致法医等需要透明决策场景下的评估不完整。

Method: 构建基准测试框架，定义“相关性”（依赖身份稳定特征）和“忠实性”（与图像内容一致且无幻觉）两个标准；开发基于结构化输出格式的方法以自动查询和审计模型输出；对多种开源VLM进行联合评估。

Result: 揭示了当前VLM生成的解释存在不足，证明仅凭准确率无法全面反映模型性能，验证了引入解释质量指标的必要性。

Conclusion: 该基准测试和开源评估工具为可解释面部识别系统的正确基准测试及未来微调奠定了基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have recently been proposed as promising tools for face recognition, as they can produce natural language explanations alongside similarity scores. This capability is considered appealing for face comparisons in forensic contexts, which require decisions to be transparent and auditable. However, existing evaluations of VLMs for that use case focus mostly on recognition accuracy, while the validity of generated explanations remains unquantified. In this work, we introduce a benchmarking framework for VLM-based face recognition that treats explanation quality as a core evaluation axis. We propose two criteria that explanations should satisfy: relevance, i.e., reliance on identity-stable facial features; and faithfulness, i.e., alignment with the visible image content without hallucinated features. We jointly develop a methodology enabling the quantification of relevance and faithfulness of evaluated models, based on constraining model outputs to a structured explanation format that supports automated querying and auditing. Using this framework, we benchmark several families of open-weight VLMs, jointly evaluating face verification accuracy and explanation quality. Our results highlight remaining shortcomings of produced explanations, and emphasize the need for such explanation quality metrics to get a complete picture of model performance. The proposed benchmark and open-source evaluation harness provide a foundation for proper benchmarking and future fine-tuning of explainable face recognition systems.

</details>


### [68] [Catena: A Comprehensive Software Suite for Large-Scale Connectomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21887)
*Samia Mohinta,Pedro Gómez-Gálvez,Shi Yan Lee,Daniel Franco-Barranco,Michael Clayton,Stephan Preibisch,Jan Funke,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Catena 是一个开源的、以开发者为中心的神经连接组学软件套件，旨在解决电子显微镜数据重建中工具碎片化、可重复性差的问题。它整合了神经元分割、突触检测等模块，通过容器化和预训练模型实现高效、可扩展且可复现的连接组映射流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经连接组重建和校对工作依赖多个分散、维护不一致或专有的软件工具，导致可重复性和自动化受阻。需要一种集成化、开放且标准化的解决方案来提高工作效率和结果的可复现性。

Method: 开发了 Catena 软件套件，包含以下核心特性：1) 集成 3D 神经元和细胞器分割、突触检测、微管追踪及神经递质推断模块；2) 采用组合式、分块处理管道设计，具备完全文档化、可扩展和适应性强的特点；3) 利用预训练机器学习模型减少计算资源和标注数据需求；4) 提供全容器化的模块，封装依赖关系以确保跨工作站和集群的一致执行。

Result: Catena 成功提供了一个统一、开放且可复现的平台，能够高效处理纳米级分辨率的电子显微镜体积数据，实现了从原始数据到细胞连接组映射的全流程自动化与标准化，显著降低了计算和数据门槛。

Conclusion: Catena 通过整合开源组件、共享模型和容器化运行时，为从电子显微镜体积数据中绘制细胞连接组提供了一种可重复且可扩展的方法，推动了神经科学数据分析和工具链的标准化进程。

Abstract: The gold standard datasets for mapping connectomes are electron microscopy volumes of densely labeled neural tissue at nanometer resolution. Yet reconstructing and proofreading neuronal arbors and annotating all synapses requires pipelining multiple software tools that are often fragmented, inconsistently maintained, or proprietary, hindering reproducibility and automation. Here, we introduce Catena, an open-source, comprehensive, developer-centric software suite for connectomics that integrates modules for 3D neuron and organelle segmentation, synapse detection, microtubule tracking, and neurotransmitter inference. Catena organizes its modules in composable, chunk-wise processing pipelines in a completely documented, extensible, and adaptable design. We further reduce compute and ground-truth data requirements with pretrained machine learning models, facilitating fine-tuning. Catena ships fully containerized modules that encapsulate evolving dependencies for consistent execution across workstations and clusters. By consolidating open components, shareable models, and containerized runtimes, Catena delivers a reproducible and scalable approach to mapping cellular connectomes from electron microscopy volumes. Code and documentation: https://github.com/Mohinta2892/catena.git

</details>


### [69] [The Role of Radiometric Features in Cross-Site Leaf-Wood Segmentation of LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21903)
*Roman Kaharlytskyi,Derek T. Robinson,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了现有激光雷达点云分割方法排除辐射特征的做法，证明在跨传感器和跨平台场景中，结合辐射特征（强度、回波数等）能显著提升木材召回率并改善结构连通性，优于仅依赖几何特征的深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的树木枝叶-木材分割方法通常为了最大化跨传感器兼容性而排除辐射特征，但其在不同扫描平台（地面与 aerial）间的泛化能力尚不明确，且可能导致结构碎片化，影响后续非破坏性生物量估算所需的定量结构模型（QSMs）重建质量。

Method: 通过在地面TLS数据集（Heidelberg）上训练模型，并在空中RPA-LiDAR数据集（Ontario, Canada）上进行测试，评估了仅使用几何特征与结合辐射特征（强度、回波数等）的分割方法在跨站点和跨平台场景下的泛化性能。

Result: 仅基于几何特征的方法（包括最先进的深度学习模型）在稀疏的空中扫描数据上泛化失败，F1分数≤0.56，且导致茎和分支组件碎片化；引入辐射特征将F1提升至0.61，木材召回率从0.16大幅增至0.35（提升119%），并保持了更好的结构连通性和连贯的建筑架构。

Conclusion: 几何模式具有视角依赖性且易过拟合扫描模式，而辐射特征编码了物理材料属性，能在不同传感器和环境间更好地泛化，从而为QSM重建提供更优的结构完整性。

Abstract: Leaf-wood segmentation of individual trees from LiDAR point clouds is essential for quantitative structure models (QSMs) used in non-destructive biomass estimation. Existing segmentation methods typically exclude radiometric features (e.g., intensity, return number) to maximize cross-sensor compatibility. We challenge this design choice by evaluating cross-site and cross-platform generalization: training on the public Heidelberg dataset (terrestrial TLS, 1550nm) and testing on a novel dataset from Ontario, Canada (RPA-LS, 905nm). Results show that geometry-only methods - including state-of-the-art deep learning models trained on high-density LiDAR datasets - fail to generalize to the sparse, top-down geometry of aerial scans, achieving F1 scores <= 0.56. Incorporating radiometric features (intensity, return number, number of returns) improves F1 to 0.61, but more critically, increases wood recall by 119% from 0.16 to 0.35. Furthermore, geometry-only approaches often result in fragmented stem and branch components. We find that leveraging radiometric features preserves greater structural connectivity, resulting in more coherent architectures that are better suited for QSM reconstruction. We demonstrate that while geometric patterns are view-dependent and prone to overfitting scan patterns, radiometric features encode physical material properties that generalize across disparate sensors and environments.

</details>


### [70] [Info3R: Information-Adaptive Test-Time Training for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21938)
*Sunghyun Baek,Hanna Bae,Minchan Kwon,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Info3R，一种针对在线3D重建的信息自适应测试时训练方法。通过信息感知状态更新和动态状态重置机制，解决长视频流处理中帧重要性和模型状态饱和问题，显著提升长序列下的相机位姿、深度估计及3D重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的在线3D重建方法在处理长图像流时，忽视了每个输入帧的重要性差异以及模型内部状态的信息饱和问题，导致长序列下性能下降。

Method: 提出Info3R方法，包含两个核心机制：1) 信息感知状态更新：根据输入帧的冗余度和信息量调节状态更新强度；2) 动态状态重置：当累积状态更新幅度达到阈值或模型预测置信度变化时触发，并结合锚点到世界坐标的对齐以恢复状态的可塑性。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计和3D重建任务上均实现一致的性能提升，显著缓解长序列评估中的性能退化。在KITTI Odometry数据集上，平均绝对误差（ATE）比LongStream降低1.68倍，展现出对扩展户外序列的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Info3R通过自适应地管理状态更新和信息重置，有效解决了在线3D重建中长序列处理的瓶颈，为实时部署提供了更 robust 的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRIME，一种通过情境记忆实现意图驱动感知注意力的反馈机制，以最小计算开销显著提升VLA自动驾驶模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在感知阶段盲视下游推理和导航目标，无法根据先前的决策优先处理关键视觉线索，导致感知与规划脱节。

Method: 引入PRIME机制，利用跨注意力聚合过去L步窗口内的潜在表示（包括感知、推理、导航目标和预测行为），构建“情境记忆”，以此条件化VLA的感知查询，实现意图驱动的感知注意力。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环基准测试中，PRIME取得82.47的最高驾驶得分（较ORION提升4.73）和60.00%的成功率（提升5.38个百分点），且仅增加最多29.7M参数（占基座模型的0.41%）。

Conclusion: PRIME通过低开销的反馈机制有效 bridging 了感知与规划的鸿沟，在Think2Drive演示训练的VLA中实现了最先进的性能表现。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [72] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*
