We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [180] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明，在单变量时间序列异常检测中，通过多模态LLM生成统计特征池并配合严格的特征选择协议，简单统计管道性能可超越现有最佳神经网络和基础模型，达到0.588的VUS-PR分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索简单的统计流水线在严格选择协议下对单变量时间序列异常检测的性能上限，并解决候选特征池对性能天花板的关键影响问题。

Method: 采用滑动窗口提取统计量，使用转导鲁棒(MAD)模型打分，并在验证集上进行特征子集选择。利用多模态LLM结合上下文示例为不同领域生成定制化的统计特征池，并与手工 crafted 池进行联合选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U数据集上，基础统计管道达到0.529 VUS-PR；结合LLM生成的特征池后，性能提升至0.588，超过最佳神经网络(0.45)和统计方法(0.44)，与排行榜最强条目持平。消融实验显示，改变候选池对性能影响最大(0.226)。

Conclusion: 候选特征池的质量决定了性能天花板，而搜索策略仅起次要作用。利用LLM自动化生成领域特定的统计特征池能显著提升简单统计方法的检测性能，使其具备与复杂深度学习模型竞争的能力。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [181] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIST模型，通过基因组引导的组织学注意力机制解决多模态生存预测中的外部队列偏移和计算复杂性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态生存模型在整合全切片图像（WSI）和基因组特征时，面临外部队列分布偏移和计算复杂度的挑战，且传统融合方法仅在最后阶段合并编码后的模态，缺乏深层交互。

Method: 提出MIST框架，将基因组特征表示为tokens，用于查询由基础模型衍生的紧凑组织学上下文tokens。训练策略结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout以及配对的WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠癌、肾癌、肺癌和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部评估队列中，MIST在主要比较中优于标准融合基线，提升了外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤预后预测策略，能有效利用分子信息与组织学上下文的互补性。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [182] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对企业生成式AI项目落地难的问题，提出EnterpriseVal评估系统，通过标准化用例、多维度指标及混合评分机制，解决从“模型能力”到“业务部署决策”的测量鸿沟，并在银行试点中验证了其在提升效率和降低幻觉方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前沿语言模型在专业任务上表现优异，但大多数企业GenAI initiative未能产生可衡量的商业效果，且大量智能体项目面临取消风险。现有公开基准测试仅回答“模型能做什么”，无法支持基于特定数据、控制和场景的“是否适合、可靠、安全且值得扩展”的部署决策，存在显著的测量缺失问题。

Method: 提出EnterpriseVal使用案例级评估系统，包含五个核心组件：(i) 用例形式化规范及冻结的社会技术配置（设定自主性与后果层级以决定评估强度）；(ii) 涵盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保证和监督的多维指标目录；(iii) 结合盲审专家判断与校准LLM-as-judge评分（通过预测驱动推断）的分级协议；(iv) 将带置信区间的指标向量映射为拒绝/有条件/扩展决策的可执行两级阈值门控算法；(v) 将审查员捕获率作为测量参数的价值与风险模型。

Result: 在全球某银行的三个工作流试点中：信用备忘录起草方面，最佳模型的引用精度达到88%（低于70%门槛），幻觉率为1.6%（低于5%门槛）；流程转化方面，分析师每份文档的修订工作量从估计的27.4小时降至2.9小时。

Conclusion: EnterpriseVal有效弥合了模型能力评估与企业部署需求之间的差距。研究区分了既定结果、试点证据、 proposed system及开放假设，并规定了全面验证所需的实验，证明了该系统在衡量AI工作流适用性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [183] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAVEL框架，一种用于交互式人员重识别的检索感知在线强化学习系统，通过优化提问策略提升部分证据下的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通过模仿离线QA对排序来训练决策，但问题价值取决于其引发的响应及对后续检索的影响，当前方法缺乏对这种动态反馈的有效利用。

Method: 引入RAVEL框架：基于监督问题生成初始化，直接观察Top-4候选者，并利用完整问答-检索循环中的排名反馈优化问题策略；利用候选区分度和感知有用性作为弱监督信号。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES数据集上的实验表明，RAVEL在五轮交互中逐步提升检索性能；分析显示其将提问预算重新分配给能提供更有用检索证据的局部开放式属性，在初始困难查询上获得最大增益。

Conclusion: RAVEL通过在线强化学习和实时反馈机制，有效解决了部分证据下交互式检索中的序列决策问题，显著提升了检索效果。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [184] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoViewMem框架，通过写入时语义解耦和结构化提取解决LLM长期记忆中的语义干扰问题，提升长程问答与个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统依赖固定粒度或静态模式，难以处理异构信息（偏好、事件、约束等）混合表示导致的语义干扰，使得Top-K检索对噪声敏感且相关证据排名不佳。

Method: 构建数据驱动的AutoViewMem框架：从交互轨迹中发现候选视图，选择紧凑互补视图集；在写入阶段进行基于溯源的结构化记忆提取，实现“表示优先”设计；并应用离线巩固以提升记忆紧凑性和一致性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和PersonaMem基准上，结合Qwen3-8B/14B骨干网络，AutoViewMem在长程问答和个性化任务上优于强基线模型，同时保持简单的推理流程。

Conclusion: 将语义解耦前置到写入阶段可有效避免检索时的噪声干扰，证明该结构化记忆组织方式能显著提升LLM代理的长期一致性与个性化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [185] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过评估运动诱导压力和跨会话变异性下的ECG生物识别，验证了内在心脏特征对生理和时间漂移具有弹性。采用Siamese ResNet结合晚期多导联融合策略，在大型数据集上实现了1.7%的会话内静息到峰值EER及CYBHi数据集3.9%的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG生物识别研究多局限于单会话、静息状态数据，缺乏对时间变异性和生理变化鲁棒性的充分测试，难以应对现实应用场景。

Method: 构建并训练一个带有晚期多导联融合策略的Siamese ResNet模型，使用从心肺运动试验中提取的大型ECG数据集进行训练，并通过结合运动和时间的感知协议及公共基准进行测试。

Result: 在会话内静息到峰值状态下达到1.7%的等错误率（EER），在CYBHi公开数据集上达到3.9%的状态-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: ECG生物识别存在固有的心脏签名，且该签名对生理和时间的漂移具有显著的弹性，证明了其在复杂现实条件下的可靠性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [186] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为PIR（内部识别探测）的方法，通过检测大语言模型内部状态来识别其“隐藏”的知识，从而区分模型是“不愿回答”还是“无法回答”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型可能持有知识但不报告的问题（如sandbagging或欺骗），仅凭输出无法判断模型是隐瞒答案还是真的不知道。

Method: 借鉴法医学中的隐蔽信息测试原理，构建PIR方法。向模型呈现问题及候选答案，从模型内部状态读取其对正确答案的识别反应。该方法无需参考模型或标注数据，具有无参考特性。

Result: 在五个系列的八个模型上，PIR平衡准确率在0.70至0.87之间，显著高于基线。即使在提示欺骗、训练性伪装、外部密码锁定等隐蔽情况下，识别率仍保持在0.85至0.93。当知识被遗忘时，信号强度降至未知水平。

Conclusion: PIR能有效分离“不愿回答”与“不能回答”的模型，支持对sandbagging行为的审计及知识遗忘验证。该信号具有因果性，补充了黑盒行为线索之外的信息，并适用于多选和自由文本生成任务。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [187] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CodeMidas，一种利用开源代码库构建强化学习环境的新方法，无需开发工件，显著提升了编码智能体在多项基准测试中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习（RL）训练编码智能体的方法通常依赖issue和commit等开发工件，限制了可提取任务的范围，难以扩展RL环境。

Method: 提出CodeMidas管道，仅以源代码为输入，通过智能体探索功能、构建执行测试、验证过滤候选任务，从3,185个开源代码库中生成5,545个训练任务。

Result: 在MiMo-V2.5模型上训练后，DeepSWE (+11.7%)、ProgramBench (+17%)、Terminal-Bench v2.1 (+8.5%) 等五大基准性能全面提升；消融实验证实高质量任务数量与性能正相关。

Conclusion: 源代码可作为构建可扩展RL环境的坚实基础，有效改善编码智能体在多样化软件任务中的行为与能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种持续适应框架，通过外部程序记忆（自然语言技能）让冻结的前端模型在专业设计软件中操作230多种工具。该记忆通过“拓宽”（获取新任务程序）和“加深”（修订现有程序）进行演化，并配合重放门控防止性能回退。在1406个真实用户简报上，无需权重更新或人工标签，GenEval2执行成功率从72.7%提升至99.3%，证明了程序记忆在噪声、不可验证反馈下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业图形设计是长视野智能体任务，涉及大量相互依赖的操作且缺乏可靠的程序化评估标准（oracle）。现有方法难以在没有明确反馈信号的情况下持续改进复杂设计流程。

Method: 引入持续适应框架：1) 使用冻结的前沿模型（如Claude-Sonnet-4/Opus-4.6）控制设计软件；2) 构建外部程序记忆库，存储自然语言描述的设计程序；3) 机制包括：拓宽（为未覆盖的重复子任务获取新程序）、加深（根据成功/失败执行经验修订现有程序）、重放门控（仅保留能修复失败且不导致已有成功退化的修改）。

Result: 在5轮迭代、1,406个真实用户简报和1,869条自动评分轨迹上，技能库从76项增至139项。GenEval2执行成功率提升11.99个百分点至99.3%。在无技能代理对比下，生成质量提升显著，且在四个专业设计基准测试中获得61.8%和67.6%的胜率。组合机制（拓宽+加深）在200个保留简报上的胜率为58.5%，显著优于单独使用任一机制（约49%）。

Conclusion: 程序记忆提供了一种实用的途径，使智能体能够在噪声大、反馈不可验证的环境下实现持续适应和能力增长，有效解决了长视野复杂任务中的技能积累与优化问题。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [SKYE: Write-Optimized Key-Value Store with Fine-Grained Control over Persistent Memory Accesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20972)
*Soujanya Ponnapalli,Sekwon Lee,Rohan Kadekodi,Vijay Chidambaram*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SKYE是一种面向持久内存（PM）的写优化键值存储，通过软件间接访问和细粒度控制，解决了现有方案高延迟但低吞吐、扩展性差的问题，显著提升了写入带宽利用率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持久内存键值存储虽然通过应用直接访问数据实现了低延迟，但由于硬件需要细粒度控制且并发写入导致性能下降，存在吞吐量低和可扩展性差的问题。

Method: SKYE采用间接访问模式：应用发送请求，由专用线程代表应用访问PM；通过软件而非硬件管理PM数据放置，利用多种媒体避免PM过载，并限制远程NUMA访问以优化扩展性。

Result: 在单个NVDIMM上，SKYE在YCSB基准测试中比最先进PM存储快2.5-5倍；在四个跨不同NUMA节点的NVDIMM配置下，SKYE达到了约86%的PM写入带宽利用率，且写入吞吐量扩展了3.9倍。

Conclusion: SKYE通过放弃硬件管理的直接访问，转而采用软件控制的间接访问和数据放置策略，成功实现了高性能、高吞吐量和良好的可扩展性的持久内存键值存储。

Abstract: State-of-the-art key-value stores built for persistent memory (PM) provide low latency as they allow application threads to directly access the data on PM and rely on hardware to manage multiple non-volatile DIMMs (NVDIMMs). While this provides low latency, it results in low throughput and scalability. Performance degrades because PM hardware requires fine-grained control over PM accesses; for example, throughput degrades if too many threads write to PM concurrently.
  We present SKYE, a write-optimized PM key-value store that achieves high throughput and scalability. SKYE builds on the central idea of maintaining fine-grained control over all PM accesses and obtains high PM write-bandwidth utilization. To achieve this, SKYE deviates from current practice and provides indirect access to applications; applications send requests to SKYE, which uses dedicated threads to access PM on their behalf. Instead of relying on hardware-managed PM, SKYE controls how data is placed on individual NVDIMMs. SKYE leverages multiple media to avoid overloading PM and limits remote NUMA accesses for scalable throughput. We show that on a single NVDIMM, SKYE outperforms state-of-the-art PM stores by 2.5-5x on the standard Yahoo Cloud Serving Benchmark (YCSB). With four NVDIMMs across four NUMA nodes, SKYE obtains about 86% of PM write bandwidth, and its write throughput scales by 3.9x.

</details>


### [190] [The Stochastic Shift: A New Evaluation Paradigm for Text-to-SQL with AI Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21133)
*Tarfah Alrashed,Fatma Ozcan,Per Jacobsson,Tal Neiman,Xianshun Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对AI增强SQL查询评估难题，提出分层评估框架，将确定性数据库逻辑与灵活AI语义解耦，显著提升评估准确率至97.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统生成的AI增强查询因AI算子的灵活性和非确定性输出，导致依赖精确结果和确定执行的现有指标失效，难以可靠评估其正确性。

Method: 形式化评估失败模式，提出多层级评估框架（Multilayered Evaluation Framework），将确定性数据库逻辑与灵活AI语义解耦，分别在工业界（BigQuery）和学术界（ThalamusDB）系统进行测试。

Result: 传统执行准确率仅能检测出25%的正确翻译；最先进的LLM自动评分器错误拒绝了32%的准确查询。所提框架在两个平台上均达到最高整体准确率（高达97.2%）。

Conclusion: 通过分别验证标准关系逻辑和AI操作，该框架为基准测试AI驱动的SQL生成器提供了可靠的标准化方案。

Abstract: SQL has been augmented with AI operators, enabling modern data analytics platforms to derive insights from both structured and unstructured data. We observe that while current Text-to-SQL systems can successfully generate these AI-augmented queries, reliably evaluating their correctness remains a critical open challenge. Current metrics, which rely on exact query results and deterministic execution, systematically fail against the flexible, non-deterministic outputs of AI operators. In this paper, we formalize these unique evaluation failure modes and introduce a Multilayered Evaluation Framework that decouples deterministic database logic from flexible AI semantics. We test our approach across both industry (BigQuery) and academic (ThalamusDB) systems. We demonstrate that traditional Execution Accuracy severely penalizes valid queries, achieving as low as a 25% detection rate for correct translations. Furthermore, even a state-of-the-art LLM-based autorater falsely rejects 32% of accurate queries due to the complexity of judging both relational and AI components simultaneously. By validating the standard relational logic and the AI operations separately, our framework achieves state-of-the-art overall accuracy across both platforms (up to 97.2%), proposing a reliable standard for benchmarking AI-powered SQL generators.

</details>


### [191] [kgsteward: a tool for building, reproducing and maintaining distributed knowledge graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21564)
*Marco Pagni,Robin Engler,Frederic Burdet,Sebastien Moretti,Loic Le Gregam,Luis Quiros-Guerrero,Jahn Nitschke,Pierre-Marie Allard,Louis-Felix Nothias,Jack McGoldrick,Ronan M. T. Fleming,Alan Bridge,Vincent Emonet,Tarcisio Mendes de Farias,Ana-Claudia Sima,Mark Ibberson,Jean-Luc Wolfender,Florence Mehl*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出kgsteward，一个基于Python的命令行工具，用于通过版本控制的配置文件自动构建和维护RDF知识图谱，解决生命科学领域整合私有与公共数据时的维护难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学协作项目需整合私有/受限联盟数据与公共参考数据库以获得统计显著性解释；尽管RDF适合此任务，但公共资源的频繁变动使得科学知识图谱的开发和长期维护极具挑战且耗时费力。

Method: 开发kgsteward工具，支持从单一版本控制配置文件在RDF存储中构建和维护知识图谱；兼容多种三元组存储，能实时更新本地图以同步外部源（包括即时转换的非RDF数据），利用SPARQL 1.1 UPDATE命令动态修正导入的RDF，并通过SPARQL查询验证图谱有效性。

Result: 该工具已在瑞士生物信息学研究所（SIB）的多个协作项目中应用，并在两个国际研究项目中验证了其适用性：一是构建包含化学分析和生物活性的植物提取物库，二是协调公共参考资源以重建人类代谢网络。

Conclusion: kgsteward有效解决了知识图谱在动态环境下的自动化构建与维护问题，促进了异构数据的集成与长期可用性，为生命科学领域的协作研究提供了可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Collaborative research projects in life sciences increasingly need to integrate private, embargoed consortium data with public reference databases in order to reach statistically meaningful interpretations. The Resource Description Framework (RDF) is well suited to this task: it facilitates the integration of heterogeneous data sources, and allows researchers to keep data and their documentation as metadata in the same place, provided the knowledge graph itself remains private during the time course of the project. Nevertheless, the development and long-term maintenance of a scientific knowledge graph remains a challenging, labour-intensive endeavour owing to the state of constant flux of most public resources. To tackle this challenge, we present kgsteward, a Python command-line tool that builds and maintains knowledge graphs inside RDF stores from a single, version-controlled configuration file. kgsteward supports multiple triplestores, keeps the local graph up-to-date with its external sources possibly already in RDF, or transformed into it on the fly, and uses SPARQL 1.1 UPDATE commands to amend further imported RDF on the fly. It can also validate the resulting graph with SPARQL queries that double as usage examples for both human users and AI agents. kgsteward has already been used in several collaborative projects at the SIB Swiss Institute of Bioinformatics, and we demonstrate its applicability in two real-world international research projects: one that builds a library of plant extracts with chemical analyses and associated bio-activities, and a second that reconciles public reference resources for human metabolic-network reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Verify, Don't Trust: Agentic Model Development for Video Discovery Retrieval at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21257)
*Hao Fu,Baiting Zhu,Minglei Chen,Yinjie Huang,Shuai Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EvoPilot，一种用于长期在线自动研究的“人工门禁”方法，通过角色专用代理、版本化技能和持久化记录来解决异步系统中常见的实验缺陷（如代码无操作、数据泄露、评估漂移等），并在YouTube视频发现系统VDD的37天实验中成功验证了交互头的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动研究循环（Autoresearch loops）多局限于短时、封闭环境，而长期的在线自动研究跨越异步系统，易因代码变更无效、数据窗口泄露、评估语义漂移或分流漏斗不一致导致结论错误，亟需一种能确保实验有效性和可追溯性的机制。

Method: 构建EvoPilot框架：1) 采用角色专用代理执行每轮实验，通过版本化领域技能和类型化适配器进行操作；2) 使用持久化记录保存实验和失败案例，并通过确定性检查强制执行已记录的教训；3) 引入人工门禁（human-gated verification）环节以验证关键结果。

Result: 在YouTube Video Deep Dive (VDD)系统的37天研究中：1) EvoPilot识别出先前自动研究将离线命中率下降归咎于交互头的错误，实为评估缺陷所致；2) 修复后测得离线提升3.20个百分点；3) 回放和突变测试排除了无效比较；4) 持久化状态恢复了中断轮次并节省约5 GPU小时；5) 7天在线随机评估显示GSRR相对提升0.66%。

Conclusion: EvoPilot通过结构化的人工干预和严格的实验追踪机制，有效解决了长期在线自动研究中的可靠性问题，能够准确识别模型改进价值并避免由工程缺陷导致的错误结论，显著提升了大规模自动化实验的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can propose, implement, and evaluate model changes. Autoresearch loops demonstrate this capability through minutes-scale iterations on a self-contained program. Online autoresearch instead spans asynchronous systems, hours-long variants, and weeks-long campaigns that can influence a product. A completed run can still support an invalid conclusion when a code change is a no-op, data windows leak, evaluator semantics drift, or the two arms traverse different serving funnels. We present EvoPilot, a human-gated method for long-horizon online autoresearch. Role-specific agents execute each round through a versioned domain skill and typed adapter. Durable records preserve experiments and failures; deterministic checks enforce recorded lessons.
  We study a 37-day campaign for the retrieval system that powers Video Deep Dive (VDD), an online experience for discovering follow-on videos after a user opens a seed video. The campaign covered seven directions and used an hourly refreshed index of hundreds of millions of videos. Earlier manual experiments had not established a benefit from an interaction head. A primitive autoresearch attempt revisited the direction but incorrectly attributed an offline hit-rate decline of 22 percentage points to the head. We then introduced EvoPilot. Its human-gated verification traced the drop to a pre-existing evaluation defect that produced output depths of 3,000 and 600. After repair, a matched comparison measured an offline improvement of 3.20 percentage points. Post-study replay and mutation tests rejected invalid comparisons while admitting valid counterparts. Durable state recovered an interrupted round, and artifact reuse avoided approximately five GPU-hours. Separately, a seven-day randomized online evaluation estimated a 0.66% relative increase in the VDD slice of Good Search Result Rate for Retention (GSRR).

</details>


### [193] [Hybrid GPU-CPU Retrieval for Personalized Search at Ultra-Large Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21281)
*Hao Fu,Jichao Sun,Baiting Zhu,Qiaoling Liu,Yan Shi,Cheng Lu,Liu Liu,Yubo Wang,Xin Yao,Xiangyu Niu,Xu Dong,Wenhan Lyu,Chiyao Shen,Yinjie Huang,Minglei Chen,Shuai Ding,Li Fan,Xiao Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“个性化-规模悖论”（深度个性化与大规模覆盖的资源冲突），通过混合GPU-CPU协同服务系统解决，实现高深度GPU路径与高广度CPU路径的互补，在万亿级文档检索中平衡性能、延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决用户生成内容在万亿级规模下检索时，深度表达性个性化需求与固定延迟/资源预算下的广泛覆盖率之间的尖锐矛盾。全量库存部署于GPU内存资源消耗过大，而CPU无法执行同等计算密集型模型以满足关键路径的延迟要求。

Method: 构建混合GPU-CPU协同服务系统，通过编排而非新模型类解决悖论：1）高深度GPU路径：对约十亿规模的精选在线池进行检索与交互预排序融合；2）高广度CPU路径：对独立选择的约二十倍大的在线库存进行轻量级个性化评分。请求可单路或双路运行，候选集去重后进入下游排序。

Result: 生产环境A/B测试显示，相比纯CPU遗留配置，系统提升了模型相关性和实质性参与度；各路径实验在其部署范围内均展现正向价值；检索日志证明路径贡献结构性不同的候选集；延迟测量和容量计划验证了将建模深度分配给GPU、库存广度分配给CPU的经济合理性。

Conclusion: 验证了一种实用的、可独立演进的“深度-广度”架构，适用于超大规模个性化搜索，有效解决了个性化与规模之间的工程悖论。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval on user-generated content at the trillion-document scale exposes a sharp conflict between two production demands: deep, expressive personalization for queries with rich user intent, and broad coverage of a massive inventory under fixed latency and resource budgets. We characterize this as the personalization-scale paradox: hosting the full serving inventory in GPU memory is too resource intensive, while CPU compute cannot execute the same interaction-heavy model on the latency-critical path.
  We present a hybrid GPU-CPU co-serving system that resolves the paradox through orchestration rather than a new model class. A high-depth GPU pathway fuses retrieval and interaction pre-ranking over a curated online pool on the order of a billion documents, while a high-breadth CPU pathway searches an independently selected online inventory roughly twenty times larger with lightweight personalized scoring. Either or both pathways can run per request; candidates are deduplicated before shared downstream ranking.
  The system is deployed in production. A full-system A/B test against the legacy CPU-only configuration improves model-scored relevance and substantive engagement, while separate pathway experiments show positive value at their own deployment scopes. Retrieval logs show that the pathways contribute structurally distinct candidates, production serving measurements characterize their latency, and a matched capacity plan quantifies the economic rationale for assigning modeling depth to GPUs and inventory breadth to CPUs. Together, these results validate a practical, independently evolvable depth-breadth architecture for ultra-large-scale personalized search.

</details>


### [194] [Auto-Bidding with Disentangled Advertiser Profiles and Train-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21308)
*Songyue Cai,Shan Gu,Wei Chen,Ziru Xu,Lianyu Wang,Jian Xu,Xiaofeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT框架，通过解耦广告主画像和免训练自适应机制，解决自动竞价中画像构建难、冷启动慢及个性化不足的问题，在大规模基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于画像的方法在推荐系统中有效，但在自动竞价领域应用受限，主要因为提取纯净画像困难、同时建模公共与私有信息复杂，且现有方法在画像更新和新广告主冷启动适应方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出ADAPT框架，包含两阶段训练范式：阶段一利用对比学习从广告主记忆库中提取静态和动态纯净画像；阶段二将动态画像解耦为公共画像和私有画像，并与静态画像结合以共同条件化竞价策略。该框架支持免训练适应，即训练后可直接为新广告主构建画像或更新现有广告主画像，无需重新训练。

Result: 在大规模自动竞价基准上的实验表明，ADAPT consistently achieves superior performance（持续取得优越性能），消融研究验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过解耦画像技术和免训练自适应机制，有效解决了自动竞价中的关键挑战，提升了个性化竞价策略的效果，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a key component of modern advertising systems that provides a personalized bidding strategy for each advertiser. By characterizing each individual, profile-based methods achieve personalization and have proven effective in domains such as recommendation. However, despite the diverse bidding behavior of advertisers, their application to auto-bidding remains limited. A primary reason is that constructing and leveraging advertiser profiles face several challenges: extracting pure profiles is non-trivial, modeling common and private information simultaneously is difficult, and profile updating and cold-start adaptation remain challenging. To tackle these issues, we propose \textbf{ADAPT}, an \underline{\textbf{A}}uto-bidding framework with \underline{\textbf{D}}isentangled \underline{\textbf{A}}dvertiser \underline{\textbf{P}}rofiles and \underline{\textbf{T}}raining-free adaptation. ADAPT introduces a two-stage training paradigm and supports training-free adaptation. Specifically, (i) the stage 1 extracts pure static and dynamic profiles via contrastive learning over the advertiser memory bank; (ii) the stage 2 disentangles the dynamic profile into a common profile and a private profile, and combines them with the static profile to jointly condition the bidding strategy; (iii) once trained, ADAPT constructs profiles for new advertisers and updates profiles of existing advertisers without retraining. Our experiments on a large-scale auto-bidding benchmark demonstrate that ADAPT consistently achieves superior performance, and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each module. The source code will be released at https://github.com/YuzunoKawori/ADAPT.

</details>


### [195] [Adaptive Preference Modeling via Explicit Indirect Relational Learning for Personalized Fashion Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21475)
*Shuiying Liao,Li Li,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出APCL框架，通过自适应聚合机制显式建模直接与间接关系，并结合对比学习提升稀疏多模态数据下的个性化时尚推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在稀疏和多模态数据条件下，难以显式建模间接的用户偏好和物品兼容性关系，通常仅依赖隐式图传播或直接交互数据，限制了模型能力。

Method: 构建相关性引导的自适应聚合机制以生成间接用户-物品和物品-物品关系；引入功能视图对比学习策略，对齐直接与间接的偏好及兼容性表示，实现统一架构下的联合建模。

Result: 在两个基准时尚推荐数据集上的实验表明，APCL一致地优于代表性基线方法，证明了其在捕捉丰富语义特征和提升稀疏交互设置鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模间接关系并结合对比学习的统一框架能有效解决个性化时尚互补推荐中的稀疏性和多模态挑战。

Abstract: Personalized fashion complementary recommendation requires jointly modeling user preferences and item compatibility under sparse and multimodal data conditions. Existing approaches often capture higher-order relational signals implicitly through graph propagation or rely on direct interaction data, limiting their ability to explicitly model indirect preference and compatibility relationships. To address this limitation, we propose an Adaptive Preference with Contrastive Learning framework (APCL) that explicitly models both direct and indirect relational signals within a unified recommendation architecture. Specifically, APCL constructs indirect user-item and item-item relationships through a correlation-guided adaptive aggregation mechanism and represents them as dedicated personalization and compatibility views. To improve representation learning, we further introduce a functional view contrastive learning strategy that aligns direct and indirect preference representations and direct and indirect compatibility representations, encouraging consistency across relational contexts. By integrating multimodal visual and textual information with explicit indirect relational modeling, APCL captures richer semantic characteristics while improving robustness in sparse-interaction settings. Experiments on two benchmark fashion recommendation datasets demonstrate that APCL consistently outperforms representative baseline methods.

</details>


### [196] [Predictable Failure in Multi-Hop Retrieval: Score-Distributional Confidence Scoring and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22056)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索置信度评分（RCS）的机制，用于解决多跳检索中的失败问题。通过证明“CWAR可归约性”和“特征区间互补性”两个理论结果，构建了无需额外LLM调用的校准放弃策略（RegimeAbstain）。实验显示该策略在多个基准测试中显著降低了自信错误答案率（CWAR），并具有良好的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索失败并非均匀分布，而是集中在结构上可预测的子群体中。现有的检索架构（如仅密集向量检索与LLM判决管道）在性能上存在差距（AUC-AC gap），且缺乏统一的理论框架来解释不同失败模式下的特征有效性差异。旨在通过形式化这些结构规律，开发更有效的置信度评估与失败规避方法。

Method: 1. 理论证明：确立了CWAR可归约性的充要条件（检索特征需包含关于成功的互信息），以及特征区间互补性（单一ANN分数特征无法在所有失败 regime 中表现最佳，需结合多种结构特征）。
2. 模型构建：实例化RegimeAbstain方法，计算检索置信度评分（RCS）。RCS是基于最多九个查询-ANN结构特征的逻辑函数，无需额外调用LLM。
3. 评估指标：定义自信错误答案率（CWAR）作为核心评估指标。
4. 实验验证：在MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA、HoVer三个多跳基准上，针对LLM-judge和dense-only两种检索架构及五种失败 regime进行评估。

Result: 1. RCS在五种条件下均达到最佳或并列最佳的AUC-AC性能，优于八个置信度基线。
2. 在MuSiQue（LLM-judge设置）上，RCS将50%覆盖率下的CWAR从39.5%降低至20.6%（相对减少47.8%），期望校准误差（ECE）为0.035。
3. 模型具有域无关性：在MuSiQue上训练的模型迁移到2WikiMultiHopQA时，AUC损失仅为-0.5pp。
4. 证实了检索特征在不同数据集和失败 regime 中具有特定的主导作用（如MuSiQue中查询长度主导，HoVer中hop-1集中度主导）。

Conclusion: 多跳检索失败具有结构化的子群体分布特征。通过利用查询-ANN结构特征构建无额外LLM开销的置信度评分（RCS），可以有效识别并规避高置信度的错误回答。该方法不仅提升了检索系统的可靠性，其背后的特征结构还表现出良好的跨数据集通用性，为优化多跳检索系统提供了新的理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval failures are not uniformly distributed across queries: they cluster in structurally predictable subpopulations. We prove two results formalizing this structure. First (CWAR Reducibility): confident-failure reduction is achievable if and only if retrieval features carry mutual information about success, a condition satisfied by LLM-judge pipelines but substantially weaker in dense-only settings, explaining the AUC-AC gap between regimes. Second (Feature Regime Complementarity): no single ANN score feature achieves best predictive performance across all failure regimes; the dominant feature differs between datasets (query length on MuSiQue, hop-1 concentration on HoVer), and a constructive witness pair shows each is necessary in one regime and non-contributory in the other. We instantiate these principles in RegimeAbstain, which computes a Retrieval Confidence Score (RCS), a logistic function of up to nine query-ANN structural features, all available without any additional LLM call, and uses it to implement a calibrated abstention policy. We define the Confident-Wrong-Answer Rate (CWAR) metric and evaluate across three multi-hop benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, HoVer) and two retrieval architectures (LLM-judge and dense-only), covering five failure regimes with CWAR from 14.5% to 62.1%. RCS achieves best or co-best AUC-AC in all five conditions against eight confidence baselines. On MuSiQue (LLM-judge), RCS reduces CWAR from 39.5% to 20.6% at 50% coverage (47.8% relative reduction), with ECE=0.035. A model trained on MuSiQue transfers to 2WikiMultiHopQA with only -0.5pp AUC loss, confirming the domain-agnostic structure of regime features.

</details>
